<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子纠错码将综合征测量时序视为固定时钟周期，但量子记忆中测量间隔本身是可优化的控制参数：测量过少会积累空闲错误，测量过多会引入测量诱导的故障，存在一个内在权衡需要被建模和优化。

Method: 提出一个现象学的逻辑噪声模型来描述测量间隔与错误累积之间的权衡；解析推导最优测量间隔与码距成反比；对于时变空闲噪声，开发基于综合征测量活动（syndrome activity）的自适应时序策略，并与匹配解码的旋转表面码模拟验证。

Result: 解析证明最优综合征测量间隔与码距成反比，且相对固定间隔方案可实现逻辑错误率随码距指数级降低；自适应策略在所有固定间隔协议下表现更优，尤其在短时强噪声突发时增益最大；表面码模拟验证了现象学模型；在Google Nature 638 (2025)报告的实验噪声参数下，预测每单位时间逻辑错误率最多降低40%。

Conclusion: 综合征测量时序不是固定不变的外在约束，而是量子记忆性能优化的重要控制维度；通过优化的固定间隔或自适应时序策略，可以显著降低逻辑错误率，为实验量子记忆的实现提供了实用的性能提升途径。

Abstract: Syndrome-measurements timing is usually treated as a fixed clock cycle of a quantum error-correcting code. For quantum memories, however, the intra-measurement interval is itself an optimizable control parameter: measuring too rarely allows idling errors to accumulate, whereas measuring too often introduces measurement-induced faults. We propose a phenomenological logical-noise model for this trade-off and analytically show that the optimal syndrome-measurements interval scales inversely proportionally with the code distance and that this produces an exponential reduction of logical-error rates in the distance relative to constant-interval schedules. Furthermore, for time-dependent idling noise, we develop an adaptive timing strategy based on the measured syndrome activity that outperforms every fixed-interval protocol, with largest gains for short but strong noise bursts. Simulations of rotated surface-code memories with matching decoding validate the phenomenological model, the distance-dependent optimum, and the adaptive-strategy improvement. Moreover, with the experimental noise parameters reported by Google in Nature 638 (2025), our model predicts reductions in logical-error rates per unit time of up to $40\%$.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作为量子驱动场下的非线性康普顿散射提供了完整而自洽的量子化描述，桥接了经典场近似与全量子处理之间的鸿沟，为理解强场QED中的量子光效应提供了新工具。

Abstract: We develop a fully quantized theory of nonlinear Compton scattering driven by a single-mode quantum field. Exact quantum-Volkov states retain pump depletion, back-action, and final-state correlations through displaced or squeezed-displaced Fock-state ladders. A finite Fock-state pump produces discrete photon-transfer edges and a terminal spectral cutoff. In the bright, weakly depleted regime, the exact theory reduces to a Wigner-function weighted-average of scattering probabilities evaluated at fixed complex field amplitudes, with ordinary and generalized Bessel functions describing the harmonic structure for circular and linear polarization, respectively. For squeezed coherent light, the squeezing angle controls the high-energy emission through photon-number fluctuations.

</details>


### [97] [Impact of Nonlinearities on Local Kinetic and Thermokinetic Uncertainty Relations in Bosonic Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06303)

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性和相互作用对局部动力学和热动力学不确定性关系（精度界）的影响，分别在谐振子网络和自旋-玻色子模型两个实验相关系统中验证了这些界在强耦合和局域活性定义下的有效性与可预测性。

Abstract: The precision of transport observables can be bounded by the entropy production and the activity of the transport process via kinetic and thermokinetic uncertainty relations. How much nonlinearities (or interactions) impact the validity and predictiveness of these local bounds and how much this impact depends on the concrete definition of activity are open questions that we address for two experimentally relevant model systems, a harmonic oscillator network and the intrinsically nonlinear spin-boson model. We thereby provide experimentally testable predictions on how nonlinearities impact or even break local kinetic and thermokinetic precision bounds.

Abstract: We apply time-reversal symmetry to quantum codes and show that it imposes parity selection rules on the physical error algebra. A time-reversal-invariant logical qubit on an odd number of spins is a Kramers doublet, forcing every even-weight Pauli to act as a scalar. Consequently, all even-weight Knill--Laflamme conditions hold automatically, so single-qubit error detection implies correction. We then reinterpret the Rains shadow enumerator through time reversal: each coefficient is a sum of error-resolved overlaps between a code and its time-reversed image.

</details>


### [99] [On Optimal Quantum Data Hiding and Maximal Separable Ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06308)
*Zhi Li*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子数据隐藏探讨的是当全局测量被限制为局部测量和经典通信时，区分能力会损失多少。该问题是量子信息论的基础问题，理解不同类别受限测量（如可分测量、LOCC、单向LOCC、无通信局部测量）的最优隐藏比率，对于量子密码学、量子通信协议设计以及理解局部性和非局部性之间的根本张力至关重要。

Abstract: Quantum data hiding asks how much distinguishing power can be lost when global measurements are restricted to local measurements and classical communication. In this work, we establish sharp results and improved bounds for several natural classes of restricted measurements. For bipartite systems on $\mathbb C^n\otimes\mathbb C^m$, we prove that the optimal data-hiding ratios against separable and LOCC measurements are both $\min\{n,m\}$. This result follows from a stronger result that, for every $2\le p\le\infty$, the largest centered Schatten $p$-ball whose associated binary measurements are implementable by finite-round LOCC has radius $\min\{n,m\}^{2/p-1}$. This strengthens the classic separable-ball theorems, while also providing an explicit finite-round LOCC implementation. For Alice-first one-way LOCC with Alice's local dimension equal to $n$, we prove that the optimal ratio is $(1+o(1))n$, with the upper bound obtained from a Gaussian rank-one POVM. For local operations without communication, we improve the universal upper bound to $(π\sqrt3/4+o(1))\min\{n,m\}$.

</details>


### [100] [Dimension-Free Polylogarithmic Quantum Shadow Tomography from Sequential Pretty-Good Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06345)
*Fernando Granha Jeronimo,Qizhao Huang,Lenny Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于阴影层析（shadow tomography）的量子协议，其样本复杂度为 O(1/ε² · (log(m/δ))⁴/(log log(m/δ))³)，对可观测量数量呈多对数依赖，且与未知量子态的维度无关，解决了Aaronson在STOC'18提出的开放问题，并相较于Sinha（STOC'25）的先前最佳维度无关样本复杂度实现了指数级改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阴影层析是量子信息理论中的基本问题。Aaronson（STOC'18）提出的开放问题：该任务是否存在与维度无关且对m仅有多对数依赖的样本复杂度？先前的下界暗示这种可能性，但一直无人给出构造性协议。

Method: 首先通过极小极大（minimax）论证将一般阴影层析问题约化为有限集合上的估计问题；然后开发一个与可观测量无关的协议，反复应用pretty-good measurement（PGM），并根据测量结果更新有限集合上的先验分布；最后通过对估计误差进行精细的尾部分析，获得对所有可观测量的同时精度保证。

Result: 样本复杂度达到 O(1/ε² · (log(m/δ))⁴/(log log(m/δ))³)，对m为多对数依赖，对维度d完全无关，同时比Sinha（STOC'25）的最佳维度无关结果实现了指数级改进。

Conclusion: 本文正面回答了Aaronson的开放性原问题，表明阴影层析确实可以在与维度无关且对可观测量数量仅多对数依赖的样本复杂度下完成，同时也刷新了维度无关场景下的最佳已知样本复杂度。

Abstract: \textit{Shadow tomography} is a fundamental problem in quantum information theory. Given multiple copies of an unknown $d$-dimensional quantum state $ρ$ and a known collection of observables ${E_1,\ldots,E_m}$, the goal is to estimate all expectation values $\{\Tr(ρE_i)\}_{i=1}^m$ to additive accuracy $\varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$.
  An elusive open question from the seminal shadow tomography work of Aaronson (STOC'18) is whether this task admits a dimension-independent sample complexity with only polylogarithmic dependence on $m$, as suggested by the best-known lower bounds. In this work, we give a quantum protocol for shadow tomography with sample complexity \[ O\left( \frac{1}{\varepsilon^2} \frac{(\log (m/δ))^4} {(\log\log (m/δ))^3} \right), \] which is polylogarithmic in the number of observables and independent of the dimension of the unknown state thereby answering Aaronson's original question while also providing an exponential improvement in the prior best dimension independent sample complexity of shadow tomography from Sinha (STOC'25).
  Our approach first reduces the general shadow-tomography problem to a finite-ensemble estimation problem via a minimax argument. We then develop an observable-independent protocol that repeatedly applies the pretty-good measurement and updates the priori distribution over the finite ensemble according to the measurement outcomes. A refined tail analysis of the resulting estimation error yields simultaneous accuracy guarantees for all observables.

</details>


### [101] [Bell nonlocality from twisted statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06359)
*Ivana Đorđ ević,Jovan Potrebić,Aleksandra Gočanin,Dragoljub Gočanin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非对易Moyal平面上自由实标量量子场的Bell关联。虽然自由场动力学和单粒子扇区不变，但形变通过扭曲多粒子统计及其Fock空间dressing表示进入。经典外部源局域耦合到扭曲dressed场，制备向两个类空分离实验室传播的动量对相干叠加态。动量依赖的扭曲相位通常不可分解，在相应波包模式间产生纠缠。研究表明适当的局域模式测量可导致CHSH Bell不等式违背，这些关联为量子场多粒子扇区中编码的非对易结构提供了操作性探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非对易空间（Moyal平面）中的量子场论是否在可观测层面留下可检测的信号，特别是通过多粒子扇区的扭曲统计来产生可操作的量子关联，从而为非对易时空结构提供实验探测途径。

Method: 在非对易Moyal平面上构建自由实标量量子场，保持自由场动力学不变但引入扭曲多粒子统计和Fock空间dressing表示；利用经典外部源局域耦合制备相干动量对态，分析扭曲相位对波包模式纠缠的影响，并通过Bell不等式检验（CHSH）来量化非对易结构产生的关联。

Result: 研究表明动量依赖的扭曲相位一般不可分解，能够在波包模式间产生纠缠；合适的局域模式测量会导致CHSH Bell不等式的违背，证明非对易结构可产生可观测的量子非局域关联。

Conclusion: 非对易性虽然不影响单粒子物理，但通过扭曲多粒子统计在纠缠生成中留下可检测的印记；Bell不等式违背为探测量子场多粒子扇区中的非对易结构提供了操作性方案。

Abstract: We investigate Bell correlations for a free real quantum scalar field on the noncommutative Moyal plane. Although the free field dynamics and the one-particle sector remain unchanged, the deformation enters through twisted multiparticle statistics and its Fock-space dressing representation. A classical external source coupled locally to the twist-dressed quantum field prepares coherent superpositions of momentum-pair configurations propagating toward two spacelike-separated laboratories. The momentum-dependent twist phases are generally nonfactorizable and generate entanglement between the corresponding wave-packet modes. We show that suitable local mode measurements lead to a violation of the CHSH Bell inequality. The resulting correlations provide an operational probe of the noncommutative structure encoded in the multiparticle sector of the quantum field.

</details>


### [102] [Structured light under turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04090)
*Guilherme S. Barros,Lucas C. Céleri,Antonio Zelaquett Khoury,André L. S. Santos Junior,Rafael M. Gomes,Guilherme L. Zanin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了拉盖尔-高斯、厄米-高斯和艾里三种光场横模结构在大气湍流扰动下的鲁棒性，通过两个空间光调制器分别实现模式生成和湍流模拟，利用相机测量强度分布并采用多种品质因数对各模式抗湍流能力进行定量比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构光被认为是高容量、高安全性的自由空间光通信的重要资源，而大气湍流是导致信号退化的主要因素。探究不同横向模式结构对湍流随机作用的抵抗力，对提升自由空间光通信的可靠性具有重要意义。

Method: 实验上使用第一个空间光调制器（SLM）对光束施加期望横向模式对应的振幅和相位调制，生成特定模式光场；第二个SLM模拟湍流介质；通过相机记录传输后的强度分布。对拉盖尔-高斯、厄米-高斯和艾里光束进行实验，并用多种品质因数（figures of merit）评估和比较各模式对湍流的鲁棒性。

Result: 获得了三种横模结构在湍流扰动下的强度分布测量结果，并通过多种品质因数对拉盖尔-高斯、厄米-高斯和艾里光束的抗湍流能力进行了定量比较。

Conclusion: 不同横向模式结构的光束对湍流表现出不同的鲁棒性，该研究为自由空间光通信中模式选择和系统设计提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Structured light has emerged as a promising resource for high-capacity and secure free-space optical communication, where atmospheric turbulence remains a major source of signal degradation. In this work, we investigate the resilience of different transverse mode structures of an optical beam with respect to the random action of turbulence. A spatial light modulator (SLM) is programmed to apply amplitude and phase modulation corresponding to the desired transverse mode, which is then transmitted through a turbulent medium emulated by a second SLM. Laguerre-Gauss, Hermite-Gauss, and Airy beams are investigated through the resulting intensity distributions measured with a camera. Different figures of merit are used to evaluate and compare the resilience of these modes.

</details>


### [103] [Observing the emergence of a velocity hierarchy in matter waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05241)
*Xudong Yu,Wenhan Chen,Igor Zhuravlev,Yi Zeng,Sudipta Dhar,Milena Horvath,Thierry Giamarchi,Laurent Sanchez-Palencia,Manuele Landini,Hanns-Christoph Nägerl,Yanliang Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在强相互作用晶格气体实验中首次观测到物质波中声速、群速度和相速度的层级分离，跨越超流-莫特绝缘体相变，并在绝缘区验证了类相对论的不变关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典色散介质中相速度和群速度自然分离，但强多体相互作用下物质波是否出现类似速度分层一直缺乏实验证据。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索速度层级是否可作为强关联量子动力学的涌现特征。

Method: 采用淬火光谱学结合时间分辨的关联测量，在跨越超流到莫特绝缘体相变的过程中，独立测量声速、群速度和相速度三个物理量。

Result: 三种速度在相变点附近近乎简并，但随着莫特能隙开启和准粒子色散获得类相对论性的巨质量形式而逐渐分离。相位相干前沿的传播速度超过Lieb-Robinson速度标度，且与局域性完全一致。在绝缘区测量到的速度满足类相对论不变关系。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了传播速度层级作为强关联量子动力学涌现特征的地位，揭示了莫特绝缘体中准粒子色散与相对论性色散之间深刻的结构相似性。

Abstract: Classical waves in dispersive media naturally exhibit distinct phase and group velocities. Whether an analogous separation of velocities can emerge in matter waves under strong many-body interactions has remained experimentally unexplored. Here, we demonstrate the emergence of a velocity hierarchy in a strongly interacting lattice gas. Using quench spectroscopy together with time-resolved correlation measurements, we independently determine the sound, group, and phase velocities across the superfluid-to-Mott-insulator transition. These velocities are nearly degenerate close to the transition, but progressively separate as the Mott gap opens and the quasiparticle dispersion acquires a massive relativistic-like form. Strikingly, phase-coherence fronts propagate faster than the Lieb-Robinson velocity scale while remaining fully consistent with locality. The measured velocities satisfy a relativistic-like invariance relation in the insulating regime. Our results establish propagation-velocity hierarchies as emergent signatures of strongly correlated quantum dynamics.

</details>


### [104] [Photon-nucleon entanglement in Compton scattering at low and high energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05330)
*Yoshitaka Hatta,Víctor Martínez-Fernández*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了康普顿散射中最终态光子-核子系统的自旋-自旋纠缠，涵盖低能（低于π介子阈值）和高能（微扰QCD到次领头阶）两个区域。作者首先证明了对于任何自旋1/2靶核，如果散射振幅为实数，则非极化康普顿散射无法产生纠缠（no-go定理）。随后研究了极化康普顿散射中电子、质子和中子靶，在低能区发现了丰富的最大纠缠Bell态及其幺正等价形式，并揭示了质子和中子靶呈现不同的纠缠模式——中子的电极化率和磁极化率对其纠缠模式和存在性有显著影响，表明纠缠可作为研究核子电磁性质的崭新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子纠缠在散射物理中的应用价值，特别是将纠缠理论引入康普顿散射领域，揭示散射过程中的量子信息特性，并建立纠缠作为探测核子电磁结构（如极化率）的新工具的可行性。

Method: 采用理论分析方法：(1) 建立no-go定理，证明实数散射振幅下非极化康普顿散射无法产生纠缠；(2) 在低能区域利用有效场论/低能展开计算极化康普顿散射的振幅，分析各运动学区域的纠缠模式；(3) 在高能区域使用微扰QCD到次领头阶计算振幅并分析纠缠结构；(4) 对比电子、质子和中子三种靶粒子的纠缠特征。

Result: 发现了低能区丰富的最大纠缠Bell态及其幺正等价形式；质子和中子纠缠模式存在显著差异；中子的电、磁极化率选择性影响纠缠模式甚至决定纠缠是否存在。这些结果首次系统揭示了康普顿散射自旋纠缠的结构及其物理参数依赖性。

Conclusion: 自旋纠缠可以作为一个新颖的物理工具来研究核子的电磁性质——特别是中子极化率对纠缠模式的强烈调制效应，使得纠缠测量有望成为传统散射截面测量之外的补充手段。

Abstract: We study spin-spin entanglement in the final state photon-nucleon system in Compton scattering, both at low energy below the pion threshold and at high energy in perturbative QCD to next-to-leading order. We first establish a no-go theorem showing that, for any spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ target, entanglement cannot be generated in unpolarized Compton scattering if the scattering amplitudes are real. We then consider polarized Compton scattering off the electron, the proton and the neutron. At low energy, we uncover a rich variety of maximally entangled Bell states and their unitary equivalents realized across different regions of the kinematic plane. Interestingly, the proton and neutron targets exhibit distinct patterns of entanglement. In the neutron case, the electric and magnetic polarizabilities dramatically influence the pattern and even the existence of entanglement. This suggests that entanglement can serve as a novel tool for investigating the detailed electromagnetic properties of the nucleons.

</details>


### [105] [Transparent boundary conditions for the spatially discrete Schrödinger equation: Reflectionless quantum transport in 1D lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05338)
*Mashrab E. Akramov,Jambul R. Yusupov,Matthias Ehrhardt,Davron U. Matrasulov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为一维离散薛定谔方程（量子晶格模型）构造了精确的透明边界条件（TBCs），利用Laplace变换解析推导出动刚度映射，得到由Bessel函数控制的卷积型边界条件，并在连续极限下验证了该离散公式与连续情形的相容性，最后通过梯形法则实现高效时间离散化，数值实验证明该边界条件完全消除虚假反向散射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子晶格等离散物理系统的数值模拟中，计算域边界处的虚假反射会严重污染解，尤其是高斯波包离开计算域时产生的伪反向散射。需要构造精确的透明边界条件以实现在有限计算区域内无反射地模拟无限域上离散薛定谔方程的解。

Method: 利用近期发展的离散系统精确解，通过Laplace变换解析地推导Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射，从而得到以Bessel函数为核的卷积型透明边界条件；在连续极限下严格证明该离散公式与连续透明边界条件的一致性；随后基于梯形法则提出高效的时间离散化方案用于实际计算。

Result: 数值实验采用Crank-Nicolson求解器验证了所提出的透明边界条件能够完全消除虚假反向散射，并保持高斯波包离开计算域时的无反射传播特性，同时验证了离散公式在连续极限下的收敛一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作为时间连续、空间离散的一维量子晶格薛定谔方程提供了解析的精确透明边界条件，兼具理论严谨性和计算可行性，为离散量子系统的无反射数值模拟奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: We construct exact transparent boundary conditions (TBCs) for a time-continuous, spatially discrete Schrödinger equation that models a one-dimensional quantum lattice. Using a recently developed exact solution for the discrete system, we derive the Dirichlet-to-Neumann maps analytically via Laplace transforms. This yields a convolution-type boundary condition governed by Bessel functions. We rigorously demonstrate the consistency of this discrete formulation with its continuous counterpart in the continuum limit. Additionally, we present an efficient time-discretization scheme based on the trapezoidal rule for practical implementation. Numerical experiments using a Crank-Nicolson solver verify that our proposed TBCs eliminate spurious backscattering entirely and preserve reflectionless propagation of a Gaussian wave packet exiting the computational domain

</details>


### [106] [School network reorganization under educational and spatial constraints using classical and quantum optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05427)
*Alessia Ciacco,Luigi Di Puglia Pugliese,Francesca Guerriero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于整数线性规划（ILP）的学校网络重组优化框架，用于解决学校规模决策问题，并通过合成基准生成器和意大利卡拉布里亚大区真实案例验证了模型的有效性，同时将其重构为约束二次模型（CQM）以适配混合量子优化环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学校网络重组是一个战略性规划问题，需要在人口趋势、地域可达性、教育需求与制度约束之间寻求平衡，同时确保公共资源的有效配置。现有的规划方法缺乏能够系统整合地理、行政和教育标准的形式化优化工具。

Method: 提出一种新颖的整数线性规划（ILP）公式化模型，整合地理、行政和教育等多维度标准；引入合成基准生成器以在人工实例上评估模型的可扩展性与计算性能；并在意大利卡拉布里亚大区使用真实机构、地域和人口数据进行案例研究。此外，将模型重构为约束二次模型（CQM）并在混合量子优化环境中实现。

Result: 该方法能够在不同政策场景下有效识别最优聚合方案，同时保持教育系统的结构性特征。合成基准测试验证了模型的可扩展性，真实案例验证了其实际适用性，量子优化实现证明了其与新兴量子技术的兼容性。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了所提方法的稳健性及其作为可持续和公平的学校网络规划决策支持工具的潜力。

Abstract: School network reorganization is a strategic planning problem that requires balancing demographic trends, territorial accessibility, educational requirements, and institutional constraints while ensuring an efficient allocation of public resources. This paper proposes an optimization framework for school dimensioning decisions based on a novel Integer Linear Programming formulation integrating geographical, administrative, and educational criteria. A synthetic benchmark generator is introduced to evaluate the scalability and computational performance of the model on artificial instances, while a real-world case study involving the complete public school network of the Calabria region (Italy) is conducted using actual institutional, territorial, and demographic data. The proposed approach effectively identifies optimal aggregation plans under different policy scenarios while preserving the structural characteristics of the educational system. Furthermore, the model is reformulated as a constrained quadratic model and implemented within a hybrid quantum optimization environment, demonstrating its compatibility with emerging quantum technologies. The results highlight the robustness of the proposed methodology and its potential as a decision-support tool for sustainable and equitable school network planning.

</details>


### [107] [Diversity in Coded TE-QKD Channels: Achieving Infinite Diversity out of Finite System Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05432)
*Shaikha S. Al-Qahtani,Siyao Li,Joseph J. Boutros*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间纠缠量子密钥分发（TE-QKD）协调过程中纠错码的无限分集特性，首次发现译码器可在信道有限分集和码字长度有限的情况下实现无限分集阶数，并从有界距离代数译码和软判决译码两个角度给出了充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TE-QKD系统的信息协调（reconciliation）过程中，如何利用纠错码提升分集阶数以降低误码率是一个基础性问题。经典衰落信道中译码只能将有限分集阶数乘以有限因子，而本研究旨在探索在TE-QKD中是否可能实现无限分集，并从理论和实例上给出严格证明与验证。

Method: 本文定义了编码TE-QKD协调中的分集阶数（高信噪比下误码率渐近斜率），并区分两种译码方式进行分析：(1) 对有界距离代数译码，推导了以每码字光子数和译码半径为变量的充要条件；(2) 对软判决译码，引入最大有限分集（MFD）性质，证明当且仅当码为MFD deficient时才能实现无限分集。最后通过Golay、RS、BCH和Reed-Muller等短码实例进行验证。

Result: 证明了在TE-QKD协调中，译码器可以在信道分集有限且码长相对较短的情况下实现无限分集阶数——这是编码与通信理论文献中前所未有的结果。该结果在经典衰落信道中没有对应物。多个短码实例验证了理论分析，并展示了系统参数和码参数对不同译码方式下可达分集的影响。

Conclusion: TE-QKD协调中纠错码可实现无限分集阶数，其充要条件分别依赖有界距离译码的光子数与译码半径关系，以及软判决译码的MFD deficient性质。这一发现突破了经典信道中有限分集的理论框架，为TE-QKD系统性能优化提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: We establish conditions and give proofs on how an error-correcting code can attain infinite diversity in a time-entanglement quantum key distribution (TE-QKD) reconciliation. The shocking result, never encountered in the literature on coding and communication theory, is that a decoder exhibits an infinite diversity order while the channel has finite diversity and the code has a relatively short finite length.
  This paper studies the diversity order of coded TE-QKD reconciliation, defined by the asymptotic slope of the error probability at high signal-to-noise ratio. For bounded-distance algebraic decoding, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition in terms of the number of photons per codeword and the decoding radius.
  For soft-decision decoding, we introduce the maximal finite diversity (MFD) property and prove that infinite diversity is achieved if and only if the code is MFD deficient. The infinite diversity in TE-QKD has no counterpart in classical fading channels, where decoding can only multiply a finite diversity order by a finite factor.
  Examples of short codes based on Golay, Reed-Solomon, Bose-Chaudhuri-Hocquenghem (BCH), and Reed-Muller codes validate the analysis and illustrate how the TE-QKD system parameters and the relatively short code parameters affect the achievable diversity for both hard and soft information reconciliation.

</details>


### [108] [Surviving correlations across a horizon: reflected entropy for bosonic fields in non-inertial frames and black hole spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05473)
*Sayid Mondal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了自由玻色子场在惯性观测者（Alice、Charlie）和均匀加速观测者（Bob）之间共享的模的反射熵和马尔可夫间隙，涵盖二分Bell态以及三分Werner和GHZ态。通过守恒荷将约化密度矩阵块对角化为精确的二维扇区，得到了三种态的闭式或半解析形式。虽然玻色子纠缠渐近消失，但Alice-Bob反射熵饱和于非零下限，仅楔间反射熵随压缩参数线性发散（与费米子情形有显著区别），而马尔可夫间隙饱和。该构造可无缝迁移到Schwarzschild黑洞，饱和值成为与质量无关的常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索反射熵和马尔可夫间隙在玻色子场中的行为，特别是与费米子情形的本质区别，并研究加速观测者与惯性观测者之间量子关联的具体特征。

Method: 利用玻戈留波夫变换描述加速观测者的模，识别守恒荷将无限维Fock空间块对角化为精确的二维扇区，从而对三种量子态（Bell、Werner、GHZ）推导出闭式或半解析的反射熵和马尔可夫间隙表达式。

Result: 玻色子纠缠虽渐近消失，但Alice-Bob反射熵饱和于非零值并收敛于Alice与Bob因果断开伙伴共享的值；只有楔间（inter-wedge）反射熵随压缩参数线性发散，与费米子情形（有界）形成鲜明对比；马尔可夫间隙全部饱和。在Schwarzschild黑洞中，饱和值成为质量无关的常数。

Conclusion: 玻色子系统的反射熵展现出与费米子系统截然不同的发散行为——楔间反射熵线性发散而马尔可夫间隙有界饱和，揭示了量子纠缠（ vanish）与经典关联（保留）在反射熵中的分离机制，且该行为具有普适性（可迁移至黑洞场景）。

Abstract: We study the reflected entropy and the Markov gap for modes of a free bosonic field shared by inertial observers (Alice, Charlie) and a uniformly accelerated one (Bob), for a bipartite Bell state and the tripartite Werner (W) and Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) states. The bosonic Bogoliubov transformation spans an infinite-dimensional Fock space with an unbounded squeezing parameter unlike the fermionic case. By identifying a conserved charge, we block-diagonalize the reduced density matrices into exact two-dimensional sectors, yielding closed or semi-analytic forms for all three states. Although bosonic entanglement is known to vanish asymptotically, the Alice--Bob reflected entropy instead saturates at a nonzero saturation floor, retaining the surviving classical correlation, and converges to the value Alice shares with Bob's causally disconnected partner. Crucially, only the inter-wedge reflected entropy diverges, linearly in the squeezing parameter---the sharp distinction from the fermionic case, where it stays bounded---while the Markov gaps saturate. The construction transfers verbatim to a Schwarzschild black hole, where the saturation values become mass-independent constants.

</details>


### [109] [Large anomalous shifts of potassium-39 Feshbach resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05512)
*Ali Zaheer,Mohammed Bouras,Krzysztof Giergiel,Rudolf Grimm,Andrei Sidorov,Peter Hannaford*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了钾-39原子在1063.9 nm光学偶极阱中Feshbach共振位置出现高达+7.5 G的异常大移动，归因于Feshbach分子异常的动态极化率，其约为两个入射原子极化率之和的4至7倍，源于1063.9 nm激光频率与分子跃迁频率的近似重合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解光学偶极阱中Feshbach共振的频移机制，特别是寻找大频移的根源，以更精确地控制超冷原子相互作用。

Method: 在1063.9 nm光学偶极阱中囚禁钾-39原子，测量33.6 G和39.9 G Feshbach共振的位置随阱深和温度的变化，从中提取Feshbach分子的动态极化率，并与理论预测比较。

Result: 观察到最大+7.5 G的共振频移；频移随阱深线性减小并趋于零；Feshbach分子极化率约为两个入射原子极化率之和的4至7倍；其他Feshbach共振无明显频移。

Conclusion: 大频移源于入射原子对与弱束缚Feshbach分子之间的差分ac Stark位移，其根本原因是Feshbach分子具有异常大的动态极化率，该极化率由1063.9 nm激光频率与分子最低三重态最后振动能级到激发三重态振动能级的跃迁频率近似共振所致。

Abstract: We report the observation of large anomalous shifts, up to +7.5 G, of the positions of the 33.6 G and 39.9 G Feshbach resonances in potassium-39 atoms confined in a 1063.9 nm optical dipole trap (ODT) at temperatures up to around 35 μK and trap depths up to about 136 μK. When the atom cloud is cooled to lower temperatures, by reducing the trap depth of the ODT, the shifts decrease proportionally with trap depth and approach zero at zero depth. We show that the large observed shifts originate from a large differential ac Stark shift between the incoming pair of potassium-39 atoms and the weakly bound Feshbach molecule, which in turn originates from an unexpectedly large dynamic polarizability of the Feshbach molecule. The polarizabilities of the Feshbach molecules extracted from the measured shifts of the 33.6 G and 39.9 G resonances are about four to seven times the sum of the polarizabilities of the two incoming potassium-39 atoms, that is, about four to seven times larger than the usual polarizability of weakly bound Feshbach molecules. The large polarizabilities of the Feshbach molecules are attributed to a near-coincidence between the frequency of the 1063.9 nm ODT laser and the frequency of a molecular transition from the last vibrational level of the lowest triplet a3Σ+u potential to a vibrational level of the excited b3Σ+g potential. Other potassium-39 Feshbach resonances we have studied exhibit a zero or very small shift, corresponding to molecular polarizabilities close to the sum of the polarizabilities of the two incoming potassium-39 atoms.

</details>


### [110] [Learning to Rank Tensor Network Contraction Plans for GPU-Accelerated Quantum Circuit Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05819)
*Alfred M. Pastor,Maribel Castillo,Jose M. Badia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习排序（Learning-to-Rank）的框架，用于在GPU上执行张量网络收缩之前，从多个候选收缩计划中高效选择最优方案。每个计划通过其逐对收缩序列的结构特征表示，并利用梯度提升排序器（结合listwise和pairwise目标）从GPU测量数据中训练。实验表明，学习到的排序器在分布内和电路族迁移测试中均优于随机和MinFill基线，且listwise模型表现最佳；在GPU架构迁移场景下，排序结果保持基本稳定，模型维持有用的决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟对量子算法的开发与验证至关重要，但其成本随电路规模急剧增长。张量网络收缩虽能利用电路结构降低成本，然而其效率高度依赖所选收缩计划。在GPU上，即使理论复杂度相近的计划，实际性能也可能因并行度、归约结构、内存流量和收缩几何的差异而显著不同。因此需要一种高效的方法在计划执行前选择最优收缩计划。

Method: 提出学习排序框架：(1) 每个收缩计划由来自其逐对收缩序列的结构特征表示；(2) 使用梯度提升排序器，分别采用listwise和pairwise目标函数，从GPU实测数据上训练；(3) 在多种电路族上进行评估，包括分布内测试和电路族迁移测试；(4) 对比随机基线和MinFill基线；(5) 额外研究后端迁移场景，即跨GPU架构的排序稳定性。

Result: 学习到的排序器总体上能识别出更好的收缩计划；listwise模型的整体决策质量最强。跨GPU架构测试中，模型排序保持基本（尽管非完全）稳定，且源设备训练的模型在目标设备上仍保持有用的决策质量。性能部分仍依赖于后端特性。

Conclusion: Learning to Rank是一种实用的方法来减少收缩计划搜索成本，同时结果也表明收缩计划的性能表现仍部分依赖后端硬件，为后续跨平台自适应优化提供了方向。

Abstract: Classical simulation remains essential for developing and validating quantum algorithms, but its cost grows rapidly with circuit size. Tensor-network contraction can reduce this cost by exploiting circuit structure, although its efficiency depends strongly on the chosen contraction plan. On GPUs, plans with similar theoretical complexity may perform very differently because execution also depends on parallelism, reduction structure, memory traffic, and contraction geometry. We present a learning-to-rank framework for selecting efficient contraction plans before executing them. Each plan is represented by structural features derived directly from its sequence of pairwise contractions, and gradient-boosted rankers are trained from GPU measurements using listwise and pairwise objectives. We evaluate the resulting models on diverse circuit families, using separate in-distribution and circuit-family-shift test sets, and compare them with random and MinFill-based baselines. The learned rankers generally identify better plans, with the listwise model providing the strongest overall decision quality. We also study backend shift by comparing empirical plan orderings on two GPU architectures and evaluating the source-trained models on the second device without retraining. The rankings remain substantially, though not perfectly, stable across GPUs, and the models retain useful decision quality. These results support Learning to Rank as a practical way to reduce contraction-plan search, while showing that performance remains partly backend dependent.

</details>


### [111] [A Scale-Invariant Theory of the Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05929)
*Julian Barbour,Maria I. R. Lourenço*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于莱布尼茨的充足理由律，提出一种相对论（关系论）本体论框架，通过消除牛顿力学中的绝对位置、方向、时间和尺度，将引力N体问题重构为仅依赖于无量纲比率的尺度不变形式，其中尺度不变流形V成为核心量，提供结构度量、形状排序和涌现的引力时间箭头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿以来，物理学保留了大量的绝对且不可观测的结构（绝对位置、方向、时间、尺度），但这些结构并非在经验上必需的。作者希望探索在多大程度上可以消除这些冗余结构，采用一种受莱布尼茨充足理由律启发的更经济的关系论本体论，从而对物理基本问题提供新的视角。

Method: 系统地依次消除绝对位置、方向、时间和尺度四类绝对结构，将标准引力N体问题重新表述为仅依赖无量纲比率的尺度不变形式。在这一框架下，引入尺度不变流形V作为核心数学对象，用于测量结构、提供形状的自然排序，并导出涌现的引力时间箭头。

Result: 该框架统一了此前被认为不同的牛顿解类别，发现了一种可能的新对称性；提出了一种基于无时间空间关联的解释概念而非时间演化；为物理学提供了一种更经济的本体论。该原理还可能为广义相对论和量子力学提供新的视角。

Conclusion: 通过系统的关系论重构，牛顿引力可以被表述为一个更经济且对称性更强的理论，其中时间本身是涌现的而非基本的。虽然该方案在牛顿引力中发展，但其原则具有推广到广义相对论和量子力学的潜力，暗示了一种更根本的、不依赖绝对时空的物理基础。

Abstract: Modern physics has achieved extraordinary empirical success while retaining much of the absolute, unobservable structure introduced by Newton, largely without questioning its necessity. We investigate how far this structure can be eliminated by adopting a relational ontology guided by Leibniz's principle of sufficient reason. Removing absolute position, orientation, time and, finally, scale leads naturally to a formulation of the gravitational $N$-body problem where only dimensionless ratios are physically meaningful. Within this framework, the scale-invariant variety $V$ becomes a central quantity, providing a measure of structure, a natural ordering of shapes, and an emergent gravitational arrow of time. We argue that the resulting formulation unifies classes of Newtonian solutions previously regarded as distinct, uncovering a possible new symmetry, suggests a notion of explanation based on timeless spatial correlations rather than temporal evolution, and points towards a more economical ontology. Although developed in the context of Newtonian gravity, the principles proposed here may also offer a new perspective on general relativity and quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [112] [Partition Functions of Hermitian and PT-Symmetric Oscillators from Integrable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06047)
*Hongfei Shu,Jingjing Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于ODE/IM对应原理，为均匀厄米与PT对称谐振子建立了热配分函数和谱zeta函数的公式化表述。量子化条件通过计数函数a(E)表达，该函数可由可积模型的Destri-de Vega方程求解；配分函数和谱zeta函数则表示为包含计数函数的围道积分，在量子谱函数与可积模型之间建立了直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上计算量子系统的热配分函数和谱zeta函数需要对能级进行求和，这在谱未知或复杂的系统中十分困难。作者旨在通过ODE/IM对应将量子谱问题与可积模型联系起来，利用Destri-de Vega方程高效求解计数函数，从而绕开直接求和的困难，为量子谱函数提供新的计算框架。

Method: 作者采用ODE/IM（Ordinary Differential Equation / Integrable Model）对应的形式体系：首先，将均匀厄米谐振子和PT对称谐振子的量子化条件转化为由计数函数a(E)描述的形式；其次，利用可积模型中的Destri-de Vega方程求解a(E)；最后，将热配分函数和谱zeta函数重新表达为涉及该计数函数的围道积分。

Result: 论文成功地给出了热配分函数和谱zeta函数以计数函数a(E)为核的围道积分表述，并利用Destri-de Vega方程实现可解性，从而构建了量子谱函数与可积模型之间的桥梁。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过ODE/IM对应，量子系统的热力学量（配分函数）和谱性质（zeta函数）可以纳入可积模型的框架中统一处理，为未来研究更广泛的量子系统提供了新的方法论工具。

Abstract: We develop an ODE/IM-based formulation for the thermal partition function and the spectral zeta function of the homogeneous Hermitian and PT-symmetric oscillators. For both classes of systems, the quantization condition can be expressed using the counting function $a(E)$, which can be solved via the Destri-de Vega equation of the integrable model. We then express the partition function and spectral zeta function as contour integrals involving the counting function, thereby providing a direct bridge between quantum spectral functions and integrable models.

</details>


### [113] [A platform for nuclear symmetry-violation searches with laser-coolable molecules carrying spinful nuclei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06138)
*Tatsam Garg,Jakob Weiß,Tesse Tiemens,Charly Beulenkamp,Andreas Schindewolf,Tim Langen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向携带相关核自旋的冷重分子系统的综合性实验工具箱，将冷却、俘获、相干态操控和精密测量协议整合为一体。以137Ba19F分子和核自旋相关宇称破缺（NSD-PV）为代表性研究对象，该方案投影统计灵敏度比现有分子束方法高出约两个数量级，可同时探测重核137Ba和轻核19F的NSD-PV信号，为核从头计算理论提供直接基准，并识别出可用于秒级转动相干性的魔幻波长，且该技术可推广至变形核分子的Schiff矩和磁四极矩测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有冷重分子宇称/CP破缺实验大多局限于核自旋为零的前驱分子种类，无法敏感于核对称性破缺现象。为探索超越标准模型的新物理，需要发展适用于携带相关核自旋的分子的实验方案，以获得增强的核自旋相关宇称破缺信号并检验核从头计算理论。

Method: 构建了一个包含冷却、俘获、相干态操控和完整精密测量协议的综合性实验工具箱，适用于携带相关核自旋的分子。以137Ba19F为代表性分子物种、核自旋相关宇称破缺（NSD-PV）为基准应用，通过整合已在现有实验中分别验证的技术来实现增强的测量灵敏度，并识别魔幻波长以实现秒级转动相干性。

Result: 该方案实现比同类分子束实验高约两个数量级的投影统计灵敏度，可实现对重137Ba核增强的NSD-PV信号以及轻19F核对NSD-PV贡献的同时测量，为核从头计算提供直接基准。此外，识别出一个候选魔幻波长，为未来实验中实现秒级转动相干性提供路线。

Conclusion: 该技术工具箱可扩展至含变形核分子的核Schiff矩和磁四极矩测量，为实验室搜索核对称性破缺现象建立通用平台，为超越标准模型的新物理探索提供现实可行的实验途径。

Abstract: Cold heavy molecules are promising systems for exploring nuclear $\mathcal{P}$- and $\mathcal{CP}$-violating phenomena in search of new physics beyond the Standard Model. However, most proposed experimental strategies and their early realizations to date have been limited to proof-of-principle molecular species with effectively spin-zero nuclei that are not sensitive to nuclear symmetry-violating phenomena. Here, we introduce a comprehensive experimental toolbox that integrates cooling, trapping, coherent state manipulation, and a complete precision-measurement protocol that is applicable to molecules carrying relevant nuclear spins. Using ${}^{137}$Ba${}^{19}$F and nuclear-spin-dependent parity violation (NSD-PV) as representative species and benchmark application, respectively, our approach achieves a projected statistical sensitivity roughly two orders of magnitude beyond comparable molecular beams by combining techniques already demonstrated individually in current experiments. This level of precision could provide realistic experimental access not only to the enhanced NSD-PV signals arising from the heavy ${}^{137}$Ba nucleus within this molecule but also to the contributions from the lighter ${}^{19}$F nucleus, bringing direct benchmarks of nuclear \textit{ab initio} theory within reach. We further identify a candidate magic wavelength as a route to second-scale rotational coherence in future experiments. The techniques developed here can be transferred to measurements of nuclear Schiff and magnetic quadrupole moments in molecules containing deformed nuclei, establishing a general platform for laboratory searches for nuclear symmetry violations.

</details>


### [114] [Dual-Faraday-laser-pumped cesium beam clock with $7.7\times 10^{-13}/\sqrtτ$ frequency stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06169)
*Xiaomin Qin,Suyang Wei,Haijun Chen,Yufei Yan,Qiang Wei,Hangbo Shi,Zhiyang Wang,Zheng Xiao,Zijie Liu,Tiantian Shi,Jingbiao Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种紧凑型双法拉第激光泵浦（DFP）铯束钟，通过低噪声原子参考激光架构克服了激光诱导的频率-幅度噪声转换限制，实现了10^-13/√τ量级的短期频率稳定度，为可部署的精密授时系统提供了高性能铯频标方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型铯束钟是可部署授时系统的主要频率参考，但其短期频率稳定度受限于时钟信噪比（SNR）。虽然双激光光泵浦能提高有效原子利用率，但激光诱导的频率到幅度噪声转换长期限制了可实现的时钟SNR，因此需要一种新架构来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出双法拉第激光泵浦（DFP）方案，采用低频率噪声的原子参考激光架构：腔内法拉第反常色散光学滤波器（FADOF）提供对Cs D2共振线的固有对准，调制转移光谱（MTS）抑制频率噪声和漂移，实现Lorentzian线宽2.12 kHz的稳键turnkey激光系统，并用于双激光泵浦铯束钟。

Result: DFP铯钟在1-Hz带宽内实现时钟SNR达46,365，分数Allan偏差为7.7×10^-13/√τ，Hadamard偏差在10,000秒时达到7.7×10^-15。

Conclusion: 该工作将紧凑型铯束钟的分数频率稳定度推进到10^-13/√τ量级，为野外部署的精密授时、导航和同步系统提供了一条通向高性能Cs频率参考的路径。

Abstract: Compact cesium beam clocks are major frequency references for deployable timing systems. However, further improvement of their short-term frequency stability is limited by the clock signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Although two-laser optical pumping can increase the effective atomic utilization, the achievable clock SNR has long been limited by laser-induced frequency-to-amplitude noise conversion. Here, we demonstrate a compact dual-Faraday-laser-pumped (DFP) Cs beam clock enabled by a low-frequency-noise atom-referenced laser architecture. The intracavity Faraday anomalous dispersion optical filter provides inherent alignment to the Cs D$_2$ resonances, while modulation transfer spectroscopy offers suppressed frequency noise and drift. The resulting laser system supports robust turnkey operation with a Lorentzian linewidth of 2.12 kHz. The DFP Cs clock achieves a clock SNR of 46,365 in a 1-Hz bandwidth and a fractional Allan deviation of $7.7\times 10^{-13}/\sqrtτ$ , with Hadamard deviation reaching $7.7\times 10^{-15}$ at 10,000 s. This work pushes the fractional frequency stability of a compact Cs beam clock into the $10^{-13}/\sqrtτ$ regime, providing a pathway toward high-performance Cs frequency references for field-deployable precision timing, navigation, and synchronization.

</details>


### [115] [Breaking Memory Bottlenecks in Quantum Control Systems for More Precise Experiments and Higher Throughput Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06318)
*Yicheng Guang,Neel Vora,Yilun Xu,Yueqi Chen,Gang Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Ant-Q是一个面向量子控制系统的内存层次结构设计，通过将DRAM与BRAM集成，支持流水线化的量子电路执行并确保电路间时序的确定性，从而解决量子控制系统的内存瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算的发展需要更精确的实验和更高的电路吞吐量来验证更多领域应用，但量子控制系统层存在内存瓶颈——片上BRAM容量有限且DRAM延迟不确定，这限制了量子设备的发展。

Method: 提出Ant-Q内存层次结构，将DRAM与BRAM集成使用，支持流水线化量子电路执行，同时确保电路间时序的确定性。

Result: 使用26个真实实验和计算电路进行评估，Ant-Q支持1Q和2Q随机基准测试的深电路，并将电路加载和读出上行相对于执行时间的开销从22.90%-1417.05%降低到接近零。

Conclusion: Ant-Q已被集成到QubiC 3.0中，部分功能已经可用，有效解决了量子控制系统的内存瓶颈问题。

Abstract: As quantum computing continues to demonstrate promise and attract growing attention, there is an increasing need for more precise experiments to advance the development of quantum devices, as well as higher circuit throughput to validate more domain applications. However, this need is hindered by a memory bottleneck at the quantum control system layer, arising from limited on-chip BRAM capacity and the non-deterministic latency of DRAM. To break this bottleneck, we present Ant-Q, a memory hierarchy design that integrates DRAM with BRAM to support pipelined quantum circuit execution while ensuring deterministic inter-circuit timing. We evaluated Ant-Q using 26 real-world experimental and computing circuits. The results show that Ant-Q supports deep circuits for 1Q and 2Q Randomized Benchmarking and reduces the overhead of circuit loading and readout uplink relative to execution time from 22.90%-1417.05% to near zero. Ant-Q is being integrated into QubiC 3.0, with part of its functionalities already available.

</details>


### [116] [The Quantum Mechanics of Rare Events: From Quantum Walks to Stochastic Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06319)
*Daniel Green,Kshitij Gupta,Akhil Premkumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了量子系统大偏差理论的实用方法，用于计算从量子游走到宇宙学中稀有的物理涨落事件。论文首先分析谐振子与热浴耦合的情形，展示系统如何从统计涨落（热涨落）演化到量子涨落主导，然后将结果推广为"稀有涨落由测量诱导的相对熵最小化所决定"的一般原理，从而为广泛的开放量子系统提供热力学描述。最后将该方法应用于随机暴涨产生的宇宙学随机游走，表明固定de Sitter背景上长波长场的密度矩阵演化破坏了KMS对称性，导致一个不满足细致平衡的定态密度矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典统计系统中的大偏差原理已阐明了半经典在稀有涨落描述中的作用，并为统计力学提供了数学基础。然而量子系统的大偏差理论发展远不成熟。由于所有物理系统本质上都是量子力学的，这在统计物理、宇宙学等领域中稀有涨落的理解上留下了重大空白。本文旨在填补这一缺口。

Method: 论文采用了以下方法论：(1) 首先分析非简谐振子与热浴耦合的具体案例，明确展示系统从统计（热）涨落向量子涨落的演化过渡；(2) 将具体结论推广到一般情形，证明主导性稀有涨落最小化测量诱导的相对熵；(3) 将上述理论框架应用于宇宙学中随机暴涨产生的量子游走问题，分析de Sitter背景上长波长场密度矩阵的演化性质，并检验KMS对称性及细致平衡条件的成立情况。

Result: 论文的定量结果包括：(1) 在谐振子-热浴系统中，清晰刻画了从热涨落主导到量子涨落主导的转变机制；(2) 建立了"稀有涨落最小化测量诱导相对熵"的广义原理，为开放量子系统提供了统一的变分/热力学框架；(3) 在宇宙学应用中，发现de Sitter背景上长波长标量场的密度矩阵演化破坏KMS对称性，其定态解不满足细致平衡条件。

Conclusion: 本文建立了量子大偏差理论的实用框架，为计算量子系统中稀有涨落提供了系统化工具。该方法统一了从介观量子系统到宇宙学随机暴涨等多种物理场景。特别地，对de Sitter背景的量子游走分析揭示了KMS对称性破缺和细致平衡失效，这对理解宇宙学中持久涨落的宏观行为具有重要物理意义。

Abstract: Rare fluctuations in physical systems depend on the detailed microphysics responsible for the fluctuations. In classical statistical systems, the large deviation principle has elucidated the role of semi-classics in describing this regime, and has simultaneously provided a the mathematical foundation of statistical mechanics. Large deviation theory for quantum system is considerably less developed. As all physical systems are fundamentally quantum mechanical, this leaves a major gap in our understanding of rare fluctuations relevant to statistical physics, cosmology, and more. In this paper, we develop the practical aspects of the theory of large deviations relevant for calculating rare events in physical systems from quantum walks to cosmology. We first analyze the case of the anharmonic oscillator coupled to a bath, showing explicitly how the system evolves from dominantly statistical (e.g. thermal) to quantum fluctuations. We then generalize these results, showing that the dominant rare fluctuations minimize the measurement-induced relative entropy. This perspective provides a thermodynamic description of a wide range of open quantum systems. We apply these results to random walks that arise in cosmology through stochastic inflation. We show that the evolution of the density matrix of long wavelength fields on a fixed de Sitter background breaks the KMS symmetry, giving rise to a stationary density matrix that does not respect detailed balance.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Entangling power of neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05293)
*Taige Wang,Nisarga Paul,Liang Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文引入编码器-解码器神经网络的"纠缠能力"这一概念，量化网络在子系统间生成纠缠的能力，该能力取决于潜空间维度K和解码器的复杂度类别。作者精确计算了多项式解码器在K维潜空间上的纠缠能力，证明即使使用适度的资源，神经网络也能展现出指数级的纠缠能力，并将纠缠理论中的Schmidt秩概念推广到机器学习相关性分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子系统间相关性的复杂度刻画是信息论、机器学习和科学领域的基础任务。在量子物理中，神经网络越来越多地被用于学习波函数，但如何量化神经网络生成量子纠缠的能力尚缺乏系统的理论框架。

Method: 引入纠缠能力（entangling power）作为编码器-解码器神经网络的度量指标，依赖于潜空间维度K和解码器的复杂度类别；对K维潜空间上作用的p次多项式解码器进行精确计算。

Result: 证明神经网络在适度资源（有限K和较小p）下即可实现指数级的纠缠能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为分析机器学习中的相关性提供了通用框架，推广了纠缠理论中的Schmidt秩概念，表明神经网络是高效的纠缠生成器。

Abstract: Characterizing the complexity of correlations between subsystems is a fundamental task across information theory, machine learning, and science. In quantum physics, neural networks have found increasing application in learning wavefunctions. Here we introduce the entangling power of an encoder-decoder neural network, which quantifies its ability to generate entanglement between subsystems, dependent on a latent space dimension $K$ and the complexity class of the decoder. We exactly calculate this quantity for polynomial decoders of degree $p$ acting on a $K$-dimensional latent space. Our results establish the exponential entangling power of neural networks with modest resources. More broadly, our work provides a framework for analyzing correlations in machine learning that generalizes the notion of the Schmidt rank in entanglement theory.

</details>


### [118] [Neural Flux Attachment: From Bose Condensates to Chiral Topological Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06168)
*Rudik Badalyan,Khachatur G. Nazaryan,Tigran A. Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出ChernFormer，一种结合费米子变压器与固定Chern-Simons相位的神经量子态架构，使每个因子的交换符号相消从而精确描述玻色子波函数。该网络能够统一描述玻色凝聚和手性拓扑液体，并在Kalmeyer-Laughlin基态及其前两个手性边缘态的基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 是否存在一个统一的神经波函数能够同时描述玻色凝聚态和手性拓扑液体？这是量子多体物理中的一个基础问题，需要在单一变分框架内同时处理常规玻色有序和手性拓扑物质。

Method: ChernFormer将费米子变压器与固定Chern-Simons相位相结合，该相位为每对粒子附加一个统计涡旋。每个因子在交换下改变符号，两者乘积恰好成为玻色子。固定相位改变统计而非概率，因此任何玻色子学习问题都等价于具有相同近似误差和重叠的费米子问题。足够容量下可逼近平面上任意固定粒子数的归一化玻色子波函数。

Result: 在一个有限光滑网络中，粒子相遇时波函数仍会消失，但接触空洞可以收缩，同时波函数和凝聚分数趋于无节点的凝聚态。在Kalmeyer-Laughlin基态和两个手性边缘态测试中，重叠曲线在最大采样尺寸下仍接近1，N=20时的独立振幅和相位映射同时恢复局部Laughlin涡旋和集体边缘涡旋。相比之下，连续非零的相同逐粒子因子乘积无法捕捉这些基本边缘扇区。

Conclusion: ChernFormer为常规玻色序和手性拓扑物质提供了统一的变分语言，能够同时描述玻色凝聚和手性拓扑液体。

Abstract: Can one neural wave function describe both a Bose condensate and a chiral topological liquid? We introduce ChernFormer, which combines a fermionic transformer with a fixed Chern-Simons phase that attaches one statistical vortex to every particle pair. Each factor changes sign under exchange, so their product is exactly bosonic. The fixed phase changes statistics but not probability, making every bosonic learning problem equivalent to a fermionic one with the same approximation error and overlap. With enough capacity, ChernFormer can approximate any normalizable bosonic wave function on the plane at fixed particle number. A finite, smooth network still vanishes when particles meet, yet this contact hole can shrink while the wave function and condensate fraction approach those of a nodeless condensate. Following the needle-in-a-haystack target-reconstruction benchmark introduced in \cite{NazaryanGaggioliTengFu2025}, we test ChernFormer on the Kalmeyer--Laughlin ground state and its first two chiral edge states. The overlap curves stay close to unity through their largest sampled sizes, while independent amplitude and phase maps at $N=20$ for all three states recover both local Laughlin vortices and the collective edge vortex. By contrast, a continuous, nonzero product of identical particle-wise factors misses these elementary edge sectors. ChernFormer therefore provides one variational language for conventional bosonic order and chiral topological matter.

</details>


### [119] [Beyond Electrons: Radiative Thermal Computing and Neural Networks at the Near-Field Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06203)
*Hexiang Zhang,Mauro Antezza,Yi Zheng*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种可编程的近场辐射热网络，通过热交换模拟卷积和循环操作，构建了热卷积神经网络（T-CNN）和热循环神经网络（T-RNN），利用VO2和GST等相变材料实现无电荷载体的热深度学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种全新的非电子计算范式，将热传递与深度学习结合，实现无需电荷载体和电路的物理学习平台，拓展传统电子神经网络的边界。

Method: 利用辐射热二极管和晶体管（基于VO2和GST相变材料）构建T-CNN和T-RNN两种基本架构。T-CNN通过温度依赖的发射率调制执行空间模式识别，每个门作为可调辐射滤波器；T-RNN嵌入辐射反馈和滞回相变以实现记忆和顺序推理。系统通过门温度分布进行外部编程，实现加权求和和非线性激活。

Result: 系统展示了三态逻辑行为、辐射增益和非易失性热存储等物理学习关键属性，成功实现了卷积、记忆保留和反馈学习的直接实施。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了首个辐射热深度学习的实现，揭示了以温度场作为智能载体的新计算范式，将传热、记忆和自适应推理统一在一个非电子系统中。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a programmable near-field radiative thermal network that emulates convolutional and recurrent operations through heat exchange. By integrating radiative thermal diodes and transistors composed of phase-change materials such as VO2 and GST, we construct two fundamental architectures: the Thermal Convolutional Neural Network (T-CNN) and the Thermal Recurrent Neural Network (T-RNN). The T-CNN executes spatial pattern recognition via temperature-dependent emissivity modulation, where each gate acts as a tunable radiative filter that amplifies or suppresses localized heat flux. The system is externally programmable via gate temperature distributions in VO2/GST, enabling weighted summation and nonlinear activation; hysteretic transitions provide state dependent responses that can be harnessed for memory. The T-RNN extends this functionality temporally, embedding radiative feedback and hysteretic phase transitions to realize memory and sequential inference. Together, these systems exhibit tri-state logic behavior, radiative gain, and non-volatile thermal storage, key attributes of physical learning. Each logical node operates without charge carriers or electrical circuits, relying solely on near-field photon tunneling between nanoscale surfaces. The resulting framework enables direct implementation of convolution, memory retention, and feedback learning within a contactless, energy-conserving platform. This study establishes the first radiative realization of thermal deep learning, revealing a new computing paradigm where temperature fields function as carriers of intelligence, uniting heat transfer, memory, and adaptive inference in a single non-electronic system.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Quality Diversity for Reliable Data Driven Time-Use Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05230)
*Aneta Neumann,Ty Stanford,Dorothea Dumuid,Frank Neumann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将预测不确定性纳入质量多样性（QD）优化框架的时间使用推荐方法。利用成分数据分析从大规模儿童队列数据中构建健康目标函数，将不确定性直接嵌入优化过程，在保持高质量行为结构的同时，将推荐向低不确定性区域倾斜，从而生成更可靠的时间分配建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每日24小时时间分配与身心健康密切相关，但现有优化方法仅关注最大化预期收益，忽略了数据驱动预测中的不确定性。忽视不确定性可能导致不切实际甚至有害的健康行为建议。

Method: 采用成分数据分析（CDA）从超过1000名儿童的队列数据中推导目标函数，建立日常活动组成与多种健康指标（BMI、生活满意度、认知能力）之间的关系。在此基础上，将预测不确定性整合进质量多样性（QD）优化框架，并通过基于变量和基于目标两种行为表征方式来探索解空间。

Result: 框架能够在不确定性嵌入下发现多样化的高质量时间使用组成，并揭示健康结果之间在不确定性条件下的显式关系。优化结果将推荐向低不确定性区域迁移，同时保留高质量结构。

Conclusion: 通过将不确定性直接嵌入优化过程，该框架为行为健康决策提供了更可靠的时间使用推荐，平衡了预期健康收益与模型置信度之间的矛盾。

Abstract: The daily allocation of the finite 24-hour time budget is strongly associated with physical, mental, and cognitive health. While predictive models can estimate the relationship between time-use compositions and health outcomes such as body mass index, life satisfaction, and cognition, most optimization approaches focus only on maximizing expected benefit and do not consider the uncertainty inherent in data-driven prediction. Ignoring uncertainty in health-related decisions can lead to unrealistic time-use recommendations. To address this gap, we introduce an uncertainty quantification Quality Diversity (QD) framework for a more reliable time-use recommendation. Objective functions are derived using compositional data analysis using a large child cohort dataset n > 1000, to capture the relationship between daily activity compositions and multiple health indicators. We develop a new approach that incorporates predictive uncertainty into QD processes and produces more reliable recommendations that balance the expected health benefits with the confidence of the model. We explore the solution space through variable-based and objective-based behavioral representations, revealing diverse high-quality time-use composition and explicit relationships between health outcomes under uncertainty. By embedding uncertainty directly into optimization, our framework shifts the time-use recommendations toward regions of lower uncertainty while preserving high-quality structures for more reliable decision-making in behavioral health.

</details>


### [121] [A Unified Causal Inference Framework for the Desirability of Outcome Ranking Paradigm in Benefit-Risk Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05244)
*Yuan Feng,Shiyu Shu,Yixin Fang,Ionut Bebu,Toshimitsu Hamasaki,Scott Evans,Guoqing Diao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的协变量校正因果推断框架，用于估计期望性结局排名（DOOR）概率，以评估随机试验和观察性研究中的获益-风险平衡。框架将DOOR概率表达为边际有序结局分布的雙线性泛函，通过序贯风险集危险度估计条件有序分布，并推导出DOOR概率的有效影响函数。模拟结果表明，交叉拟合的目标最大似然估计（CVTMLE-SL）在点估计和基于EIF的推断方面均表现最佳。该方法在抗菌药物耐药性领导集团的耐多药微生物网络数据中得到了实证应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医学研究中，获益-风险评价至关重要，而传统的因果推断方法大多关注单一结局指标，难以全面反映多种临床结局的综合价值。DOOR概率作为一种序贯结局排序指标，能够更全面地评估不同治疗策略的获益-风险平衡，但目前缺乏统一的协变量校正因果推断框架来估计这一指标。

Method: 该框架将DOOR概率表达为两治疗策略下边际有序结局分布的雙线性泛函；通过序贯风险集危险度估计条件有序分布；推导DOOR概率的有效影响函数以支持推断。在模拟中比较了G-computation、归一化IPW、AIPW和TMLE等多种估计方法，结合GLM或Super Learner估计干扰函数，并评估有无交叉拟合的EIF推断表现。

Result: TMLE-SL在点估计性能上最强且最一致，AIPW-SL次之。在EIF推断评估中，CVTMLE-SL在DOOR尺度偏倚、底层有序分布恢复、标准误精度和置信区间覆盖率方面均表现出最强综合性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架为因果框架下的DOOR概率估计和推断提供了有效工具，CVTMLE-SL是推荐的首选估计方法。该方法在抗菌药物耐药性研究中的实际应用进一步验证了其实用价值。

Abstract: We developed a unified covariate-adjusted causal inference framework for estimating the desirability of outcome ranking (DOOR) probability for benefit-risk evaluation in randomized trials and observational studies. The framework expresses the DOOR probability as a bilinear functional of the marginal ordinal outcome distributions under the two treatment strategies, estimates conditional ordinal distributions through sequential risk-set hazards, and derives the efficient influence function (EIF) of the DOOR probability. The point-estimation simulations compared G-computation, normalized inverse probability weighting (IPW), augmented IPW (AIPW), and targeted maximum likelihood estimation (TMLE), with nuisance functions estimated using generalized linear models or Super Learner (SL). TMLE-SL showed the strongest and most consistent point-estimation performance, with AIPW-SL ranking second. EIF-based inference was then evaluated for AIPW-SL and TMLE-SL, with and without cross-fitting, across settings varying in overlap, treatment-effect heterogeneity, and treatment allocation. CVTMLE-SL showed the strongest overall performance across DOOR-scale bias, recovery of the underlying ordinal distributions, standard-error accuracy, and confidence-interval coverage. We illustrate the methodology using data from the multidrug-resistant organism network of the Antibacterial Resistance Leadership Group.

</details>


### [122] [Deep Generalised Mixed Models: a Novel Neural Network Structure for Analysing Hierarchical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05930)
*Nina van Gerwen,Dimitris Rizopoulos,Manon Hillegers,Loes Keijsers,Sten Willemsen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"深度广义混合模型"（Deep Generalised Mixed Model）的新型神经网络架构，将传统混合效应模型推广至深度学习框架，用于分析经验采样法（ESM）收集的纵向高维数据，尤其针对青少年情绪日记数据中因缺失导致的偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的统计方法在处理高维ESM数据时存在扩展性瓶颈，而标准机器学习方法无法处理因非随机缺失（如青少年因强烈负面情绪而退出研究）导致的偏差，亟需一种既能灵活建模又能处理缺失数据机制的新方法。

Method: 提出Deep Generalised Mixed Model，该架构通过固定效应和随机效应半参数化地灵活建模数据的均值与相关结构；在估计上采用变分自编码器（VAE）的改进版本以及贝叶斯数据增强算法，使其能够处理广义分布的纵向结果、扩展到高维场景，并在数据随机缺失（MAR）时提供有效推断。

Result: 将该模型应用于GrowIt!研究及多项模拟实验，结果显示模型具备潜力，但由于模型不稳定性导致性能欠佳（suboptimal performance）。

Conclusion: Deep Generalised Mixed Model为纵向高维ESM数据分析提供了一种有前景的深度学习方案，能够处理实时缺失和灵活建模，但目前模型稳定性不足，需要在后续研究中进一步改进其估计算法和架构设计以提升实际可用性。

Abstract: The experience sampling method (ESM) is a longitudinal research design where participants report their thoughts, emotional states and behaviours multiple times a day. Our work is motivated by such data collected by the GrowIt! app, which was released to investigate daily emotions among adolescents during the COVID-19 pandemic. Current procedures to analyse ESM data face various challenges. While standard statistical techniques may not scale well to a high-dimensional setting, machine learning procedures can give biased results due to selection bias introduced by missingness. In our motivating dataset, adolescents dropped out due to previous strong feelings of negative emotions. Hence, the implied missing data are of the missing-at-random type that standard machine learning procedures cannot accommodate. We develop a novel neural network architecture that generalises mixed effects models to deep learning to overcome these challenges. It allows semi-parametric and flexible modelling of data's mean and correlation structure through fixed and random effects. For estimation, we use an adaptation of variational auto-encoders and a Bayesian data augmentation algorithm. Through this approach, the model can accommodate longitudinal outcomes following generic distributions, scale well to high-dimensional settings and provide valid inference when data are missing-at-random. We applied the Deep Generalised Mixed Model to the GrowIt! study and various simulations. The results show potential for the Deep Generalised Mixed Model, yet suboptimal performance due to model instability.

</details>


### [123] [Handling Missing Data in Probabilistic Regression Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06195)
*Taiane Schaedler Prass,Alisson Silva Neimaier,Guilherme Pumi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出三种扩展概率回归树（PRTrees）以直接处理缺失预测值的方法，无需预先插补。实验表明，缺失值处理策略（fill strategy）对预测性能的影响大于平滑分布或代理选择准则，在缺失比例高的数据集上经常优于CART。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典回归树和概率回归树在处理缺失预测值时通常依赖预先插补，这增加了流程复杂性并可能引入偏差。作者希望将缺失值处理直接整合进PRTree的树构建过程中，使模型能够在任意缺失模式下保持原有的概率性质（概率守恒和边际相容性），同时提升预测性能。

Method: 在PRTree框架中提出三种缺失值处理策略：(1) 均匀概率方法（uniform-probability），对所有可能分裂分配均匀概率；(2) 部分观测方法（partial-observation），仅利用可观测信息进行概率分配；(3) 降维平滑方法（dimension-reduced smoothing），在降维后的空间中执行平滑分裂分配。三种方法均保持原始方法的概率守恒和边际相容性，适用于任意缺失协变量模式。

Result: 在多个具有不同缺失程度的真实数据集上，提出的方法与经典回归树（CART）对比。结果表明：缺失观测的处理方式（fill策略）对预测性能影响最大，其作用超过平滑分布和代理选择准则；在缺失比例较大的数据集中，所提方法经常优于CART，同时保留树模型的解释性和灵活性。

Conclusion: 将缺失预测值的处理直接融入概率回归树的构建过程是可行且有效的。缺失处理策略的选择是影响模型性能的关键因素，PRTree扩展方法在缺失数据场景下提供了优于CART的替代方案，兼顾了预测精度与模型可解释性。

Abstract: Probabilistic Regression Trees (PRTrees) are a smooth and consistent alternative to classical regression trees, producing continuous predictions through probabilistic split assignments. This paper extends the PRTree framework to accommodate missing predictor values directly during tree construction, eliminating the need for prior imputation. Three strategies are proposed, each exploiting the available information differently: a uniform-probability approach, a partial-observation approach, and a dimension-reduced smoothing approach. These modifications are defined to preserve the fundamental probabilistic properties of the original methodology, including probability conservation and marginal compatibility, under arbitrary patterns of missing covariate values. The proposed methods are evaluated on several real-world datasets exhibiting different levels of missingness and are compared with classical regression trees. The results show that the effectiveness of probabilistic tree construction depends strongly on the treatment of missing observations. Across the considered datasets, the fill strategy emerged as the dominant modeling component, often exerting a larger influence on predictive performance than either the smoothing distribution or the proxy-selection criterion. In datasets where a substantial proportion of observations contained missing predictor values, the proposed methods frequently outperformed CART, while maintaining the interpretability and flexibility of tree-based models.

</details>


### [124] [Beyond Marginal Validity: Finite-Sample Guarantees for Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06206)
*Anton Conrad,Rustam Isaev,Denis Belomestny,Eric Moulines,Sergey Samsonov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为随机局部化共形预测（RLCP）提供了首个有限的、在 realized 局部邻域上同时控制条件覆盖有效性与 oracle 效率的有限样本高概率保证，并分解了带宽偏差-方差权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测虽然提供有限样本、分布自由的边际覆盖保证，但边际有效性可能掩盖严重的协变量特定的校准错误。精确保留条件覆盖在有限样本下不可达，RLCP 通过局部校准缓解了这一差距，但现有理论缺乏对 realized 局部集合同时控制条件有效性和 oracle 效率的有限样本保证。

Method: 对任意固定得分，在条件得分 CDF 的 Hölder 正则性、标准密度和核假设下，证明在 realized 局部邻域上均匀成立的条件覆盖缺口与相对 oracle 的长度误差的高概率界。界分解为 O(h^β) 的局部化偏差与随校准规模递减的校准项。同时分析数据分割学习的得分场景（如共形分位回归），将均匀局部保证分解为固定得分校准与均匀得分估计误差。

Result: 获得了 uniform over realized localization neighborhood 的条件覆盖缺口与长度误差的高概率界，揭示了带宽的偏差-方差权衡及 RLCP 何时能追踪 oracle 的清晰条件；在枢轴得分目标下，改进的学习可锐化局部化保证。

Conclusion: 该理论首次系统地桥接了 RLCP 局部校准实用性与有限样本理论基础，阐明了带宽选择与得分学习对局部条件有效性的量化影响。

Abstract: Conformal prediction endows arbitrary black-box predictors with finite-sample, distribution-free marginal coverage, yet marginal validity can hide severe covariate-specific miscalibration, while exact distribution-free conditional coverage is finite-sample unattainable. Randomly localized conformal prediction (RLCP) mitigates this gap by calibrating near the test point while preserving marginal coverage. Existing theory, however, lacks finite-sample guarantees for the realized localized set that jointly control conditional validity and oracle efficiency. We provide such guarantees. For any fixed score, under Hölder regularity of the conditional score CDF and standard density and kernel assumptions, we prove high-probability bounds, uniform over a realized localization neighbourhood, for the conditional-coverage gap and the length error relative to the oracle. The bounds decompose into an $O(h^β)$ localization bias and a calibration term decreasing with calibration size, clarifying the bandwidth bias-variance tradeoff and when RLCP tracks the oracle. We also analyze data-split learned scores: when the score targets a pivotal score, as in conformalized quantile regression, uniform local guarantees decompose into fixed-score calibration and uniform score-estimation errors, showing that improved learning sharpens localized guarantees.

</details>


### [125] [Minimax Optimal Early-Stopped Gradient Descent for Gaussian Mixture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06250)
*Alex Buna,Shirley Xiaoqi Liu,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了过度参数化分类中，在标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型下，对逻辑损失进行梯度下降时，提前停止（early stopping）可以实现极小极大最优的过剩零一风险，且显著优于线性插值分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在过度参数化分类中，训练数据即使底层分布不可分，也往往是线性可分的。此时梯度下降在范数上发散但方向上收敛到最大间隔插值分类器，其隐式偏置在统计上是次优的。作者旨在证明提前停止可以克服这种次优性，实现统计上最优的风险。

Method: （1）在带标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型下，对提前停止的梯度下降迭代给出尖锐的上界；（2）对任意分类器建立匹配的统计下界，从而得到最优收敛速率；（3）提出一个新的校准定理，将过剩逻辑风险转换为过剩零一风险，该定理处理了标签翻转噪声引起的模型错配，并去除了标准界中的平方根率；（4）建立线性插值器的下界，证明其需要指数级更多的样本才能达到同等过剩风险。

Result: 在协方差谱具有快速且连续衰减（包括多项式与指数衰减）的情况下，提前停止的GD在适当oracle时刻达到极小极大最优的过剩零一风险；线性插值器则可能需要指数级更多样本才能达到相同风险。

Conclusion: 提前停止是克服过度参数化分类中隐式偏置统计次优性的有效手段，论文的校准结果也为处理模型错配下的风险转换提供了新工具。

Abstract: In overparameterised classification, training data can be linearly separable even when the underlying distribution is not. In this setting, gradient descent (GD) on the logistic loss diverges in norm while converging in direction to a max-margin interpolating classifier, whose implicit bias can be statistically suboptimal. In this work, we show that early stopping can overcome this suboptimality: in a Gaussian mixture model with label-flipping noise, GD stopped at an appropriate oracle time achieves minimax-optimal excess zero-one risk for covariance spectra with fast and continuous decay, including polynomial and exponential spectral decays. Our analysis combines a sharp upper bound for the early-stopped iterate with a matching statistical lower bound over arbitrary classifiers, yielding optimal rates that are validated by experiments. A central technical contribution is a new calibration result that converts excess logistic risk into excess zero-one risk; it handles the model misspecification induced by the label-flipping noise, and removes the square-root rate in standard bounds. We also establish a lower bound for linear interpolators, showing that interpolation can require exponentially more samples than early stopping to achieve the same excess risk.

</details>


### [126] [Stochastic Dynamics on Persistence Diagram Space via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06276)
*Farzana Nasrin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个强化学习框架，用于持久性图（PD）空间上的随机动力学建模。通过拓扑感知的局部编辑操作定义受控马尔可夫过程，建立不可约、非周期和几何遍历条件以保证唯一平稳分布的存在，并通过奖励函数引导动力学实现分布匹配、任务特定拓扑统计和结构保持压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的持久性图统计分析通常将图视为静态对象，缺乏在PD空间上进行概率建模和随机演化的框架。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一种既能建模PD随机动态、又能引导其朝向科学相关拓扑目标演化的统一框架。

Method: 1) 通过拓扑感知的局部编辑操作定义PD空间上的受控马尔可夫过程；2) 建立马尔可夫链不可约性、非周期性和几何遍历性的条件，确保唯一平稳概率分布存在；3) 设计多目标奖励函数，涵盖分布匹配、任务特定拓扑统计和结构保持压缩；4) 利用强化学习优化动力学策略。

Result: 在合成数据和人脑神经影像PD上的实验表明，该框架能够在降低图复杂度的同时保持主导拓扑结构，验证了其用于自适应拓扑简化和概率建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了PD空间上随机动力学的首个强化学习框架，兼具理论保证（几何遍历性）和实际应用价值（拓扑简化与概率建模），为持久性图的动态分析和自适应处理开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Persistence diagrams (PDs) provide stable and interpretable summaries of multiscale topological structure. While substantial progress has been made in the statistical analysis of PDs, existing literature often treats diagrams as static objects and provide limited frameworks for probabilistic modeling and stochastic evolution on PD space. We introduce a reinforcement learning framework for stochastic dynamics on PD space, where diagrams evolve through topology aware local edit operations. The dynamics define controlled Markov processes on spaces of finite PDs with variable cardinality. We establish conditions under which the induced Markov chains are irreducible, aperiodic, and geometrically ergodic, implying the existence of unique stationary probability laws on PD space. To guide the dynamics toward scientifically relevant topological targets, we formulate objectives that encompass distribution matching, task specific topological statistics, and structure-preserving compression. The resulting rewards balance task specific distributional targets, diagram fidelity, and complexity reduction, and yield a framework for adaptive topological simplification and probabilistic modeling. Experiments on synthetic and neuroimaging PDs demonstrate that the proposed framework can preserve dominant topological structure while reducing diagram complexity.

</details>


### [127] [Optimal Rates for Learning with Monotone Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06337)
*Anay Mehrotra*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究单调对手注入额外正确标注样本后的学习问题，证明对于VC维≥2的类别，最小最大期望错误率从PAC最优的Θ(d/n)退化为Θ((d/n)log(n/d))，即额外正确标注样本会使学习变难一个对数因子，且该额外代价是内在的而非特定算法伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Larsen等人提出了单调对手模型：对手在观察干净样本后插入有限个正确标注的例子，使组合样本不再可交换。他们证明了ERM在该模型下达到O((d/n)log(n/d))的错误率，且每个已知的最优学习器都会被推离Θ(d/n)最优率。他们提出的开放问题是：额外的对数因子究竟是特定算法的伪影，还是缺乏可交换性的内在后果？本文旨在解答这一根本性问题。

Method: 本文通过上下界匹配来证明最坏情况下的最小最大错误率。上界方面：对d=1给出简单的不当学习器，其分析借用单包容图（one-inclusion graph）的留一法（leave-one-out）论证。下界方面：所有下界来自单一构造——构造一个显式类别及先验，使得两个仅在非平凡质量点上不同的目标假设产生相同的样本，以此证明无法区分这两个假设，从而推导出维度d≥2时无法超越Θ((d/n)log(n/d))的界。

Result: 证明在单调对手注入模型下，最坏情况下最小最大期望错误率为：d=1时Θ(1/n)，d≥2时Θ((d/n)log(n/d))。相同的率在将VC维替换为Littlestone维d_L时同样成立，因此干净的在线到批量（online-to-batch）转化率O(d_L/n)也无法实现。这确认了额外对数因子是该模型的固有性质。

Conclusion: 额外正确标注的例子（即使对手受限于有限插入预算）会使学习难度增加一个对数因子，这一结论甚至对具有有限错误界的在线可学习类别也成立。维度一的优化率由简单的不当学习器实现，而所有更高维度的下界来自一个统一的构造。该结果揭示了PAC学习中交换性要求的关键作用。

Abstract: A monotone adversary observes an i.i.d. labeled sample and appends a finite number of further examples of its choice, every one of them labeled correctly by the target hypothesis. The learner sees a uniform shuffle of the combined sample and is scored on the original distribution. Every example is correctly labeled, but the insertions depend on the clean sample, so the combined sample is not exchangeable. Larsen, Pabbaraju, and Shetty, who introduced this model, showed that empirical risk minimization attains expected error $O((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for classes of VC dimension $d$, and that every known optimal learner can be pushed away from the $Θ(d/n)$ rate, optimal for PAC learning. They asked whether the extra logarithm is an artifact of those particular algorithms or an inherent consequence of the lack of exchangeability.
  We show that this additional cost is inherent beyond VC dimension one. In the worst case over classes of VC dimension $d$ and over known finite insertion budgets, the minimax expected error is $Θ(1/n)$ at $d=1$ and $Θ((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for $d\geq 2$. The same rates hold with Littlestone dimension $d_{\mathrm L}$ in place of $d$, so the clean online-to-batch rate $O(d_{\mathrm L}/n)$ is unattainable as well. Thus, somewhat counterintuitively, adding correctly labeled examples can make learning harder by a logarithmic factor, even for classes that admit finite mistake bounds in online learning. The dimension-one upper bound is achieved by a simple improper learner whose analysis adapts the leave-one-out argument underlying the one-inclusion graph. All of our lower bounds are elementary and come from a single construction: an explicit class and prior on which two target hypothesis, which differ a point of nonnegligible mass, produce the same sample.

</details>


### [128] [Scalable estimation of VARMA models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06340)
*Daniel Paulin,Victor Elvira*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使VARMA模型估计的计算成本与序列长度T无关的框架，通过偏自相关重参数化保证平稳性和可逆性、高斯先验引入正则化、以及基于Parseval恒等式的充分统计量损失函数，在近线性截断成本下获得两个点估计器，使VARMA在大维场景下变得实用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VARMA模型长期被认为在中高维度下不实用：似然函数非凸、参数化仅等价类可识别、每次评估需遍历整个序列。然而MA项能用少量参数捕捉纯AR模型需多滞后才能匹配的动态。现有VAR类方法虽然在实践中广泛使用，但缺乏MA项的建模能力，因此需要一种计算上可行的大规模VARMA估计方法。

Method: 框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 偏自相关重参数化，从构造上保证平稳性和可逆性；(2) 对角线和非对角线条目分别设置尺度的Gaussian先验；(3) 损失函数仅依赖于固定大小的充分统计量，通过Parseval（Fourier）恒等式以近线性截断成本求值。由此导出两个点估计器：正则化最小二乘拟合和协方差边缘化的最大后验估计器。该机制可扩展至季节动态、外生回归变量（VARMAX）及滚动窗口重拟合。

Result: 两个估计器在固定维度下以近参数速率恢复真实过程的无限自回归表示，截断不引入渐近偏差。经验上，从d=10到d=40估计器保持接近oracle预测误差（此时经典条件MLE返回不可逆拟合导致预测发散），并在零售需求、气象和空气质量数据上匹配或超越VAR、贝叶斯VAR、逐分量ARMA和稀疏VARMA基线。

Conclusion: 该框架使基于似然的VARMA估计在每次迭代成本与序列长度无关的条件下，达到实践中历来依赖VAR模型的问题规模，消除了VARMA计算屏障，为大规模时间序列建模提供了一种兼具MA项表达能力和计算可行性的新方案。

Abstract: Vector autoregressive moving-average (VARMA) models have long been considered impractical beyond moderate dimensions: the likelihood is non-convex, the parametrization is identified only up to equivalence, and every evaluation costs a pass over the entire series. Yet their moving-average term captures with a few parameters what a pure autoregression matches only with many lags. We introduce an estimation framework that removes this computational barrier: each optimization iteration is independent of the series length $T$. The framework combines a partial-autocorrelation reparametrization that guarantees stationarity and invertibility by construction, Gaussian priors on the reparametrized coefficients with separate scales for diagonal and off-diagonal entries, and losses that depend on the data only through fixed-size sufficient statistics, evaluated by a Parseval (Fourier) identity at near-linear cost in the truncation length. This yields two point estimators: a regularized least-squares fit and a covariance-marginalized maximum-a-posteriori estimator. We prove that both recover the infinite-autoregressive representation of the true process at a near-parametric rate in fixed dimension, so the truncation introduces no asymptotic bias. The same machinery extends, at the same leading cost, to seasonal dynamics, exogenous regressors (VARMAX), and rolling-window refits. Empirically, the estimators stay close to the oracle forecast error from $d=10$ to $d=40$ (where classical conditional MLE returns non-invertible fits whose forecasts diverge) and match or beat VAR, Bayesian-VAR, component-wise ARMA, and sparse-VARMA baselines on retail-demand, meteorological, and air-quality data. This brings likelihood-based VARMA estimation, at a per-iteration cost independent of the series length, to the problem sizes where practitioners have so far relied on VAR models.

</details>


### [129] [FlowAdam: Implicit Regularization via Geometry-Aware Soft Momentum Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06652)
*Devender Singh,Tarun Sheel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowAdam——一种混合优化器，将Adam与基于常微分方程（ODE）的连续梯度流积分相结合。当EMA统计量检测到优化景观困难时，FlowAdam自动切换到截断的ODE积分模式。核心贡献是"软动量注入"（Soft Momentum Injection）机制，在模式转换时融合ODE速度与Adam的动量，避免朴素混合方法导致的训练崩溃。在耦合优化基准上（低秩矩阵/张量恢复、Jester协同过滤），FlowAdam的隐式正则化使留出误差降低10-22%和6%，超越调优后的Lion和AdaBelief；在良态负载（CIFAR-10）上与Adam相当；消融实验证明软注入不可或缺（硬替换使准确率从100%降至82.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam等自适应矩方法使用基于梯度二阶矩指数移动平均的对角、坐标方向预条件器，这种对角缩放依赖坐标系，在处理稠密或旋转的参数耦合时（如矩阵因子分解、张量分解、图神经网络）表现不佳，因为它独立处理每个参数。现有方法无法有效处理参数间的耦合关系，需要一种能够捕捉参数关联的优化策略。

Method: FlowAdam采用混合优化策略：1) 基座使用Adam进行自适应矩更新；2) 通过EMA统计量检测优化景观的困难程度，当检测到困难时切换到截断的ODE积分（连续梯度流）模式；3) 提出Soft Momentum Injection机制，在Adam模式与ODE模式之间的转换过程中，将ODE速度与Adam动量进行软性融合，防止模式切换导致的训练崩溃；4) 通过混合正则化实现更好的泛化性能。

Result: 在低秩矩阵/张量恢复任务上留出误差降低10-22%，在Jester真实协同过滤数据集上降低6%，超越调优后的Lion和AdaBelief；在良态条件（CIFAR-10）下与Adam性能相当；MovieLens-100K验证了收益来源于耦合参数交互而非偏差估计；消融实验显示硬替换动量会从100%降至82.5%准确率，证明软注入的关键性。

Conclusion: FlowAdam通过将Adam与ODE连续性梯度流结合并引入软动量注入机制，有效解决了Adam在处理参数耦合问题时的短板。该混合优化器在不牺牲良态条件下性能的同时，大幅提升了耦合优化任务（矩阵/张量恢复、协同过滤、图神经网络等）的泛化能力，为复杂结构化优化问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Adaptive moment methods such as Adam use a diagonal, coordinate-wise preconditioner based on exponential moving averages of squared gradients. This diagonal scaling is coordinate-system dependent and can struggle with dense or rotated parameter couplings, including those in matrix factorization, tensor decomposition, and graph neural networks, because it treats each parameter independently. We introduce FlowAdam, a hybrid optimizer that augments Adam with continuous gradient-flow integration via an ordinary differential equation (ODE). When EMA-based statistics detect landscape difficulty, FlowAdam switches to clipped ODE integration. Our central contribution is Soft Momentum Injection, which blends ODE velocity with Adam's momentum during mode transitions. This prevents the training collapse observed with naive hybrid approaches. Across coupled optimization benchmarks, the ODE integration provides implicit regularization, reducing held-out error by 10-22% on low-rank matrix/tensor recovery and 6% on Jester (real-world collaborative filtering), also surpassing tuned Lion and AdaBelief, while matching Adam on well-conditioned workloads (CIFAR-10). MovieLens-100K confirms benefits arise specifically from coupled parameter interactions rather than bias estimation. Ablation studies show that soft injection is essential, as hard replacement reduces accuracy from 100% to 82.5%.

</details>


### [130] [The Loss Does Not See the Basis, but Adam Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05136)
*Devender Singh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个关键发现：梯度下降对因子化模型W=UV^T隐式偏向低秩解，而Adam从相同小初始化出发则不具备此性质。其根源在于损失函数的规范对称性(U,V)→(UQ,VQ)。梯度流实现低秩恢复的机制仅在优化器满足规范等变性（gauge-equivariant）时才可用。梯度下降、动量、"共享标量"Adam、Muon和Shampoo满足该性质；Adam、RMSProp等逐坐标方法不满足。结构定理证明无记忆等变规则恰好等价于Gram决定型左预条件器，传递定理将梯度流的路径性质推广至共享标量流。作者在欠定矩阵感知任务上对比九种更新规则，发现从逐坐标到共享标量预条件的单参数族可单调恢复低秩偏差，证明了各向异性是根源。此外提出"谱调度"调和关于Muon的两份矛盾报告，并在Transformer实验中证明Adam在第一步就分离两个规范等价的初始化，最终导致逐头不变量W_Q^T W_K相对Frobenius距离相差56%。在两组高光谱数据集上，匹配训练损失时梯度下降在最低采样密度下将留出误差降低43-44%，且有效秩更低。基的选择因此不是调参细节，而是决定优化器选择何种插值器的关键决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同优化器（如梯度下降与Adam）在因子化模型中对低秩解的隐式偏差差异，并解释其根本机制。这一差异直接影响优化器在下定矩阵感知等欠定问题中恢复真实解的能力，对深度学习中优化器选择与泛化性能有深远影响。

Method: 从规范对称性视角出发，定义优化器的规范等变性条件，并用结构定理刻画所有满足等变性的无记忆更新规则（等价于Gram决定型左预条件器）。通过传递定理将梯度流的路径性质推广到共享标量流。随后在欠定矩阵感知任务上对比九种更新规则的恢复误差，构造从逐坐标到共享标量预条件的单参数插值族验证各向异性为根源，并提出"谱调度"解释Muon的性能差异。最后在Transformer和高光谱数据集上进行实证验证。

Result: 梯度下降、动量、共享标量Adam、Muon和Shampoo具备规范等变性，能保留低秩机制；Adam、RMSProp等逐坐标方法不具备。在Transformer中，Adam在第一步即分离两个规范等价的初始化，最终导致W_Q^T W_K相对Frobenius距离相差56%。在高光谱数据上，梯度下降在最低采样密度下将留出误差降低43-44%，且有效秩更低。各向异性预条件是破坏低秩偏差的根本原因。

Conclusion: 优化器的基选择（coordinate vs. shared-scalar preconditioning）不仅是一个数值实现的细节，而是决定优化器在欠定问题中选择何种插值器的本质决策。规范等变性是优化器继承梯度流低秩机制的必要条件，各向异性是破坏该机制的根本原因。这一发现为设计具有理想隐式偏差的优化器提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Gradient descent on a factored model $W = UV^\top$ is implicitly biased toward low-rank solutions, while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, is not. We trace the difference to the gauge symmetry of the loss, its invariance under $(U, V) \mapsto (UQ, VQ)$. Gradient flow's low-rank mechanism is available to an optimizer only if that optimizer is gauge-equivariant, a condition necessary for the transfer but not sufficient for low-rank recovery. Gradient descent, momentum, "shared-scalar" Adam, Muon, and Shampoo satisfy it. Adam, RMSProp, and the other coordinate-wise methods do not. A structure theorem characterizes the memoryless equivariant rules as exactly the Gram-determined left preconditioners, and a transfer theorem carries gradient flow's pathwise properties to common-scalar flows. We then sort nine update rules on underdetermined matrix sensing by recovery error against the planted ground truth. A one-parameter family from coordinate-wise to shared-scalar preconditioning restores the bias monotonically, isolating anisotropy as the cause. A "spectral schedule" reconciles two opposing reports about Muon: equal-rate updates recover exactly low-rank targets but lose their edge as the spectral tail grows. In transformers, Adam separates two gauge-equivalent initializations at the first step, where the equivariant optimizers stay at float precision, and ends with the per-head invariants $W_Q^\top W_K$ 56% apart in relative Frobenius distance, a gap no per-head rotation can close. On two hyperspectral datasets at matched training loss, gradient descent cuts held-out error by 43-44% at the lowest sampling density, and at lower effective rank. Basis choice is therefore not a tuning detail but a decision about which interpolant the optimizer selects.

</details>


### [131] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示因子化生成模型中常见的风格潜变量边缘分布正则化并不等同于类别独立性。作者通过精确分解证明，仅匹配边缘分布无法约束类别条件分布，导致潜变量仍可高度预测类别标签。实验表明，尽管全局MMD接近零，线性探针仍能以74%-100%的准确率恢复类别标签（随机水平10%），且该泄漏在多种扰动下持续存在。四种缓解策略可将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但仍未解决类内依赖问题。最终结论是：仅基于风格潜变量边缘分布的散度无法证明类别独立性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化生成模型通常假设将风格潜变量z_s的边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验即可实现风格表示与类别信息的独立。作者指出这一常见解释在数学上是不正确的——边缘分布匹配对类别条件分布没有任何约束，因此需要推导精确的分解条件并实证验证这一泄漏问题的普遍性和严重性。

Method: 作者首先推导出因子化采样所需的四条件精确分解，证明消除边缘-条件失配是必要条件但非充分条件。随后通过案例分析模型和四个潜在基线进行实证，使用全局MMD和线性探针评估泄漏；在六个独立扰动（模型容量、课程学习、先验几何、监督方式，两个数据集）下验证泄漏的持续性；提出四种缓解策略，并测试了后验条件先验和经验风格库两种后处理方法。

Result: 案例模型和四个基线实现了接近零的全局MMD，但线性探针仍能以74%-100%的准确率恢复类别标签（10%为随机水平）。模型达到99.15%的聚类准确率，而外部评估的类条件生成仅16%成功。泄漏在六种扰动下持续存在。四种缓解策略将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类内依赖基本不变。后验条件先验在MNIST上达到0.97（无需重训练），在CIFAR-10上仅0.41；经验风格库在CIFAR-10上达到0.88。

Conclusion: 仅基于风格潜变量边缘分布计算的任何散度都无法证明与类别标签的独立性，仅报告边缘统计量不足以验证因子化生成模型中通常声称的属性。这要求研究者和实践者重新审视因子化生成模型的验证标准。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [132] [Risk-Aware Quantile Learning for Personalized Dynamic Treatment Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05434)
*Chunyin Lei,Annie Qu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Risk-Aware Quantile Dynamic Treatment Regimes (RQDTR)，一个在分位数动态治疗方案框架下同时优化疗效分布尾部并控制治疗风险的统一框架，支持多类别治疗方案，并通过角度化公式联合学习决策规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床序贯决策往往不仅需要最大化平均疗效，还需同时优化疗效分布的相关尾部特征、控制治疗相关风险，并在多个治疗方案间进行选择。现有分位数动态治疗方案(DTR)方法虽能捕获治疗结局的分布特征，但大多局限于仅考虑疗效的目标和二元治疗方案，无法充分满足实际临床需求。

Method: RQDTR框架包含三个可解释子类：仅疗效的分位数学习、基于约束的群体风险控制学习和通过复合获益-风险效用构建的效用学习。通过角度化公式实现对多类别治疗方案的决策规则联合学习。理论上建立了识别性与oracle等价性、平滑替代函数的Fisher一致性、估计治疗方案的相合性以及有限样本误差率。

Result: 通过大量模拟实验以及应用于All of Us重度抑郁症和MIMIC-III脓毒症数据的实证分析，RQDTR相比现有分位数DTR方法在尾部导向疗效上表现更优，并实现更有利的获益-风险权衡。
