Conclusion: RQDTR为临床序贯决策提供了一个统一的、风险感知的分位数优化框架，能够同时兼顾疗效分布尾部优化与风险控制，并扩展至多类别治疗方案，具有坚实的理论保障和良好的实际表现。

Abstract: Sequential clinical decision-making often involves more than maximizing average efficacy. Clinicians may need to simultaneously optimize clinically relevant tails of the outcome distribution, control treatment-related risk, and choose among multiple treatment options. Existing quantile dynamic treatment regime (DTR) methods capture distributional features of treatment outcomes but remain largely restricted to efficacy-only objectives and binary treatments. To address these limitations, we propose Risk-Aware Quantile Dynamic Treatment Regimes (RQDTR), a unified framework that optimizes a prespecified quantile of the cumulative potential outcome while explicitly incorporating treatment-related risk. We also develop an angle-based formulation for jointly learning decision rules across multiple treatment categories. Our framework includes three interpretable subclasses: efficacy-only quantile learning, constraint-based learning with population-level risk control, and utility-based learning through a composite benefit-risk utility. Theoretically, we establish identification and oracle equivalence, Fisher consistency of the smoothed surrogate, consistency of the estimated regime, and finite-sample performance error rates. Extensive simulation studies and applications to All of Us major depressive disorder and MIMIC-III sepsis data demonstrate that RQDTR improves tail-oriented efficacy and achieves more favorable benefit-risk trade-offs than existing quantile DTR methods.

</details>


### [133] [Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotopes for Identifiable and Refinable Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05454)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出混合概率多面体（HProbZ）输出头，将预测中的三类不确定性——离散模式选择、有界系统漂移和不可约随机噪声——分别表示为zonotope的二元、有界和随机生成器，并通过卷积获得闭式似然。HProbZ共享有界生成器以代数方式耦合跨步预测，在单次前向传播中实现观察一步精化所有后续步的预测分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测头要么输出高斯混合，要么输出单一符合区域，二者都无法区分真实预测任务中常见的多种不确定性来源。这些来源本质不同，需要分别建模以提高预测的可解释性和精度。

Method: HProbZ将三类不确定性编码为zonotope的三种生成器：二元生成器（离散模式选择）、有界生成器（系统漂移）和随机生成器（随机噪声）。通过卷积推导闭式似然；共享有界生成器使预测步骤间产生代数耦合，实现单次前向传播中的条件精化；推理时利用共享结构提供解析的逐模式风险与无分布的多模态符合集。

Result: 理论方面证明了三种生成器可由似然确定到置换意义下可识别，且HProbZ密度与任何有限高斯混合在表示上不同。实证方面，在代表性预测基准上相比同编码器混合基线表现有效，同时提供了混合或凸符合预测器无法联合提供的结构性质。

Conclusion: HProbZ提供了一种统一且结构可解释的概率预测框架，能够同时建模和分离多种不确定性来源，兼具理论可识别性与实际预测性能，填补了高斯混合与符合预测方法之间的空白。

Abstract: Probabilistic prediction heads in neural networks typically output either a Gaussian mixture or a single conformal region. Neither separates the distinct sources of uncertainty often present in real prediction tasks: a discrete choice among modes, bounded systematic drift within the chosen mode, and irreducible stochastic noise. We introduce the Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope (HProbZ), an output head that represents these three sources as binary, bounded, and stochastic generators of a zonotope, and admits a closed-form likelihood by convolution. Sharing the bounded generator across prediction steps couples future predictions algebraically, so observing one step refines the predictive distribution at every remaining step in a single forward pass. We establish that the three generators are identifiable from the likelihood up to permutation, and that an HProbZ density is representationally distinct from any finite Gaussian mixture. The same shared structure provides analytic per-mode risk and distribution-free multi-modal conformal sets at inference time. Empirical analysis on representative prediction benchmarks supports the effectiveness of the design relative to same-encoder mixture baselines, while offering structural properties that mixture or convex-conformal predictors do not jointly provide.

</details>


### [134] [Innovation-Residual Auditing of Autonomous Analysis Agents: Localization, Detection Limits, Error Control, and Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05490)
*Ahmed Hassoon,Mark Dredze*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对基于无标签数据的自主数据分析审计方法进行了理论分析，揭示了评分函数选择决定错误能否被定位，给出了控制虚警率的程序及其在模型不完美、选择偏差下的退化程度，并证明了错误幅度低于某一阈值时根本无法归因的根本极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体现在可以独立完成整个数据分析流程（选队列、连表、拟合模型），但当分析出错时，需要定位是哪个操作导致的。已有方法无需标注错误数据，仅从正确分析中学习并标记偏离模型预测的操作，但其可靠性从未被系统研究过。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者从理论上分析评分函数的选择对错误定位能力的影响：对比基于单步条件概率的评分（前一步操作的条件惊讶度）与基于更长重建序列的评分方式，量化错误在评分中的扩散程度，并讨论误差渐进累积时比较长度的选取。随后提出了控制单个被审计分析中虚警比例的程序，其假设仅要求正确分析具有可交换性而非拟合模型正确，并量化了模型不完美和内容依赖的选择性审查对保证的削弱程度。最后推导出任何审计方法可归因错误的幅度下限。

Result: 单步评分下，继承性错误与正确操作不可区分，一个错误只产生一个标记；基于长重建的评分则把一个错误扩散到多个操作。所提程序能在单次审计中控制虚警比例，但保证在模型不完美或选择依赖内容的审查下会减弱。更重要的是，错误低于某幅度时完全无法归因，该极限随正确分析样本量增加而下降极慢——在当前的表示规模下，样本量增加一百倍也只能将该极限降低不到百分之二，表明表示维度才是根本瓶颈而非训练数据量。

Conclusion: 自主数据分析的审计可靠性受评分函数选择、表示维度与样本量的三重约束。其中表示维度是主要瓶颈，单纯增加训练数据几乎无法改善错误归因的下限，这为现有审计方法的能力边界提供了严格的理论刻画。

Abstract: Autonomous agents now carry out entire data analyses, selecting cohorts, joining tables, and fitting models with little step-by-step supervision. When such an analysis turns out to be wrong, someone must determine which operation caused it. A recent approach does this without any labelled mistakes, learning instead from analyses known to be sound and flagging operations that depart from what that model predicts; how reliable such audits are has not been studied. This paper supplies that analysis. The choice of score determines whether an error can be localized at all. If each operation is scored by how surprising it is given the operation immediately preceding it, then operations that merely inherit an earlier error are indistinguishable from correct ones, so one mistake produces one flag; scores computed against a longer reconstruction of the intended analysis instead spread a single mistake across many operations. We quantify how far they spread, and how to choose the comparison length when an error accumulates gradually rather than at once. We then give procedures that control the proportion of falsely flagged operations within a single audited analysis, requiring only that sound analyses be exchangeable rather than that the fitted model be correct, and we quantify how much the guarantees weaken when the model is imperfect or when the analysis was selected for review in a way that depends on its content. Finally we establish a limit on what any such audit can report: errors below a certain magnitude cannot be attributed at all, being indistinguishable from ordinary variation among sound analyses. This limit falls so slowly as more sound analyses are collected that at the representation sizes now in use a hundredfold increase reduces it by under two percent, so the dimension of the representation rather than the volume of training data is the binding constraint.

</details>


### [135] [Structured Dimension-Matched Joint Variational Transdimensional Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05607)
*Pingping Yin,Xiyun Jiao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化维度匹配变分传递推断（SM-VTI），用于有限可枚举模型空间上的贝叶斯模型选择。通过构造图将模型表达为局部stop/child决策序列，每个类型化边将科学化的父子编辑编译为精确的、基于原生坐标的维度匹配提升，边缘条件流学习残差连续传输，从而定义一个直接联合变分分布，避免了饱和最大维度代理空间的嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯模型选择需要同时处理离散模型指示符和模型特有的连续参数空间，而传统变分推断方法要么将所有模型嵌入到饱和的最大维度代理空间中（失真且浪费计算），要么难以在离散与连续混合空间中有效学习联合分布。因此需要一种能够直接在原生模型空间上进行传递推断的方法。

Method: SM-VTI包含三个核心组件：(1) 根构造图：将每个模型表达为局部stop/child决策的序列；(2) 类型化边编译：将声明的科学父子编辑转换为精确的原生坐标维度匹配提升，避免嵌入饱和最大维度空间；(3) 边缘条件流：学习残差连续传输。最终由局部策略和条件流定义单一联合变分分布，推导其精确路径密度并优化联合反向KL目标。

Result: 在受控的15模型目标上，SM-VTI-Joint成功恢复了终末质量（terminal masses）、局部动作和非线性条件几何。在128模型的错误设定鲁棒变量选择问题中，在10个数据集上采用几乎参数匹配的仿射比较，与AVTI相比，SM-VTI在相同目标评估预算下表现出更强的早期模型质量恢复能力和具有竞争力的最终联合精度。

Conclusion: SM-VTI提供了一种不依赖饱和最大维度代理的传递变分推断范式，通过构造图与原生坐标维度匹配的有机结合，在模型质量恢复效率和最终联合推断精度上均展现出优越性或至少与现有方法（如AVTI）相当的性能，特别适用于需要从有限模型中依据科学编辑关系进行选择的任务。

Abstract: Bayesian model selection couples a discrete model indicator with a model-specific continuous parameter space. We introduce structured dimension-matched variational transdimensional inference (SM-VTI) for finite enumerable model spaces. A rooted construction graph expresses a model as a sequence of local stop/child decisions. Each typed edge compiles a declared scientific parent-child edit into an exact native-coordinate dimension-matching lifting; an edge-conditioned flow then learns the residual continuous transport. The resulting local policy and conditional flow define one direct joint variational distribution, without embedding every model in a saturated maximum-dimensional surrogate. We derive its exact path density and optimize the joint reverse-KL objective. On a controlled 15-model target, SM-VTI-Joint recovers terminal masses, local actions, and nonlinear conditional geometry. On a 128-model misspecified robust variable-selection problem, a 10-data-set nearly parameter-matched affine comparison with AVTI shows stronger early model-mass recovery and competitive final joint accuracy under the same target-evaluation budget.

</details>


### [136] [Fuzzy network jump models for soft dynamic clustering of graph-structured data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05786)
*Federico P. Cortese*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模糊网络跳变模型（fuzzy network jump model），用于对加权图中节点索引的时变观测进行聚类。该框架通过空间和时间正则化促进连接节点之间及相邻时间点上的平滑软聚类分配，并采用高效的交替优化方案进行估计。模拟研究表明该方法能准确恢复真实归属概率并优于竞争方法，应用于旧金山交通网络数据可识别出可解释的交通状态及其时空演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的许多时变观测数据（如交通流量、传感器数据）具有图结构且随时间演化，传统聚类方法难以同时捕捉节点间的空间依赖和跨时间的状态变化。需要一种既能处理时空结构又允许软聚类分配的模型，以更灵活地表征聚类边界的模糊性和状态的动态演化。

Method: 提出模糊网络跳变模型（fuzzy network jump model），将模糊聚类与网络结构相结合，通过引入空间正则化项（促进连接节点的聚类分配平滑）和时间正则化项（促进相邻时间点聚类分配的连续性）构建目标函数；利用正则项的二阶（二次）结构设计高效的交替优化（alternating optimization）算法进行参数估计。

Result: 模拟研究涵盖不同空间依赖程度和聚类重叠水平，结果显示所提方法能准确恢复真实的成员概率，并在聚类性能上优于现有竞争方法。对旧金山城市交通网络数据的应用成功识别出具有可解释性的交通状态，并揭示了这些状态在时间维度和相连道路段上的演化模式。

Conclusion: 模糊网络跳变模型为时空图数据的软聚类提供了一种有效且可扩展的解决方案，兼顾了空间结构与时间动态的平滑性约束，兼具理论上的优化效率与实践中的可解释性，在交通分析等应用中具有广泛潜力。

Abstract: We introduce a fuzzy network jump model for clustering time-varying observations indexed by the nodes of a weighted graph. The framework allows flexible graph representations with spatial and temporal regularization promoting smooth soft cluster assignments across connected nodes and consecutive time points. Estimation is performed through an efficient alternating optimization scheme that exploits the quadratic structure of the regularization terms. A simulation study covering different levels of spatial dependence and cluster overlap shows that the proposed method accurately recovers the true membership probabilities and outperforms competing clustering methods. An application to traffic-network data for the city of San Francisco identifies interpretable traffic regimes and reveals their evolution over time and across connected road segments.

</details>


### [137] [Verifiable Regularity Criterion for Conditional Expectation Operators and Conditional Mean Embeddings with Applications to Nonparametric Regression, Bayesian Inverse Problems, and Koopman Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06155)
*Maximiliano Hertel,Ilja Klebanov,Manuel Schaller,Karl Worthmann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究条件期望算子（CEO）及其条件均值嵌入（CME）的函数空间映射性质，证明该映射性质由条件分布的Radon-Nikodym密度的正则性刻画，并给出CEO有界且Hilbert-Schmidt的简洁可验证充分条件。对于与Sobolev空间范数等价的RKHS，该条件简化为条件密度的Sobolev正则性。作者在非参数回归、贝叶斯逆问题和随机动力系统的Koopman算子理论三个场景中验证了该正则性条件，为CME表示和Galerkin型估计器的误差界提供了统一验证路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件期望算子在应用数学和机器学习中广泛存在（非参数回归、贝叶斯逆问题、Koopman算子理论），但其将Y上的函数空间映射到X上特定函数空间（尤其是RKHS）的性质尚未被系统刻画。核心问题是：何时CEO的映射性质可以保证？这直接关系到CME表示的合法性和估计器的误差界。

Method: 本文通过条件分布的Radon-Nikodym密度的正则性来刻画CEO的映射性质。具体地：（1）建立了CEO有界且Hilbert-Schmidt的充分条件；（2）当RKHS与Sobolev空间范数等价时，该条件简化为条件密度的Sobolev正则性；（3）在三个典型场景（非参数回归、贝叶斯逆问题、随机动力系统的Koopman算子理论）中，利用经典概率模型的正则性结果验证所需映射性质。

Result: 成功建立了CEO映射性质与条件密度正则性之间的等价关系，给出了简洁可验证的Hilbert-Schmidt有界性充分条件，并在三个应用场景中均验证了该正则性条件成立，表明经典正则性结果即可推导出所需的映射性质。

Conclusion: 该框架为条件期望算子的映射性质提供了统一视角，连接了概率论、算子理论、核方法和随机动力系统等多个领域，为CME表示和Galerkin型估计器的误差分析提供了直接且可验证的路径。

Abstract: Conditional expectation operators (CEOs) and their associated conditional mean embeddings (CMEs) play a central role across applied mathematics and machine learning, appearing in nonparametric regression, Bayesian inverse problems, and Koopman operator theory. A fundamental question is when a CEO maps a function space on $\mathcal{Y}$ into a prescribed function space on $\mathcal{X}$, particularly a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS). We show that such mapping properties are characterized by the regularity of the Radon--Nikodym density of the conditional law, and establish a simple, verifiable sufficient condition under which the CEO is bounded and Hilbert--Schmidt. For RKHSs norm-equivalent to Sobolev spaces, this condition reduces to Sobolev regularity of the conditional density. The result yields a direct route to validate CME representations and error bounds for Galerkin-type and CME-based estimators. We verify the regularity condition in three settings: nonparametric regression, Bayesian inverse problems, and Koopman operator theory for stochastic dynamical systems. We show in each case that classical regularity results on the underlying probabilistic model imply the required mapping properties. The resulting framework offers a unified perspective on conditional expectation operators across probability, operator theory, kernel methods, and stochastic dynamics.

</details>


### [138] [The Tamed Subgradient Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06283)
*Iosif Lytras,Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-TULA算法，用于处理势函数同时非光滑、超线性梯度增长且非凸的目标分布的采样问题。与AdamW和Muon相比，其坐标提升变体在GPT-2系列大语言模型预训练上表现具有竞争力，并提供非渐近收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于次梯度的Langevin算法在非光滑、超线性与非凸三重要求并存时收敛速率不够理想；同时，实际应用（如大语言模型预训练）中的目标函数往往同时具有这些特性，但缺乏具有非渐近理论保证的采样方法。

Method: 提出Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA)：直接作用于次梯度以避免计算昂贵的平滑步骤；采用taming技术处理超线性梯度增长，产生稳定的显式离散化格式。推导Wasserstein-2距离下的非渐近收敛界，所有常数以维度和逆温度显式追踪。还提出坐标提升变体用于LLM预训练。

Result: 相比现有基于次梯度的Langevin算法，SG-TULA获得更优的收敛速率；提供优化问题对应的excess risk估计；在GPT-2系列LLM的regularized预训练势上验证了假设（含显式常数），坐标提升变体在预训练中对finetuned AdamW和Muon具有竞争力。

Conclusion: SG-TULA为同时具备非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸性的目标分布提供了一种兼具理论保证与实用竞争力的采样算法，尤其在大语言模型预训练场景中展现应用前景。

Abstract: We study the problem of sampling from target distributions whose potentials are simultaneously non-smooth, subject to superlinear gradient growth, and non-convex. We introduce the Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA), a discretisation of the Langevin diffusion that operates directly on subgradients, without relying on computationally demanding smoothing procedures. To handle the superlinear regime, taming techniques are employed to produce a stable, explicit scheme. We derive non-asymptotic convergence bounds in Wasserstein-2 distance, with all constants tracked explicitly in terms of dimension and inverse temperature, improving upon the currently known rates for subgradient-based Langevin algorithms. We further provide excess risk estimates for the associated optimisation problem. We verify the assumptions, with explicit constants, for the regularized pretraining potential of a LLM in the GPT-2 lineage and the boosted coordinate-wise variant of SG-TULA pretrains the former competitively against finetuned AdamW and Muon, for which no comparable non-asymptotic guarantees are presently available.

</details>


### [139] [Surv-IPTB: An Attention-Based Model for Estimating Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit with Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06288)
*Lev V. Utkin,Stanislav K. Kogan,Andrei V. Konstantinov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Surv-IPTB，一种基于注意力机制的框架，用于在生存分析中估计个体治疗获益概率（IPTB），通过将IPTB估计重新表述为二分类问题，利用成对患者比较和区间值概率处理右删失数据，在多个非线性合成数据集上优于T-learner和S-learner等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生存分析中，估计个体患者接受治疗相对于对照能获得更长生存时间的概率（IPTB）对于个性化医疗决策至关重要，但现有方法在处理复杂非线性特征空间和右删失数据时性能显著退化，缺乏一个可扩展且统计上规范化的解决方案。

Method: Surv-IPTB框架将IPTB估计转化为二分类任务，通过治疗组和对照组之间的成对患者比较来实现；采用不精确概率表示（区间值概率）原则性地处理右删失观测；利用带有可学习查询-键变换的注意力机制实现数据驱动的成对比较聚合，同时对删失病例学习软类别概率。

Result: 在具有复杂非线性结构的合成数据集（螺旋形、钟形、环形特征空间）上的大量实验中，该模型在不同删失率和治疗效果强度下保持稳健性能，持续优于配备随机生存森林、Cox比例风险和Beran估计器的元学习基线（T-learner和S-learner），尤其是在非线性挑战场景下优势显著。

Conclusion: 基于注意力的框架被证明是一种可扩展且统计上规范化的个性化治疗获益评估方案，代码已公开可用。

Abstract: This work presents a novel attention-based framework for estimating the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB) in survival analysis contexts. The proposed model, called Surv-IPTB, directly quantifies the probability that a specific patient will experience extended survival time under treatment versus control. We reformulate IPTB estimation as a binary classification problem, leveraging pairwise patient comparisons across treatment and control cohorts. The framework incorporates a principled handling of right-censored observations through imprecise probability representations, where uncertain treatment effects are characterized by interval-valued probabilities. An attention mechanism with learnable query-key transformations enables flexible, data-driven aggregation of pairwise comparisons, while simultaneously learning soft class probabilities for censored cases. Through extensive experiments on synthetic datasets with complex nonlinear structures, including spiral, bell-shaped, and circular feature spaces, we demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance across varying censoring rates and treatment effect strengths. The model consistently outperforms meta-learner baselines (T-learner and S-learner) equipped with random survival forests, Cox proportional hazards, and Beran estimators, particularly in challenging nonlinear scenarios where conventional methods exhibit significant degradation. The results establish the proposed attention-based framework as a scalable and statistically principled solution for personalized treatment benefit assessment in survival settings. The code implementing the model is publicly available.

</details>


### [140] [Learning Latent Memory States from Longitudinal Athlete Monitoring Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06290)
*Dae-Jin Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出"潜在记忆表"（Latent Memory Table）作为纵向数据的新分析单元，与主成分得分矩阵、随机效应表等并列的可复用统计对象。通过记忆算子将掩码窗口历史映射为有限维状态并附带不确定性，形成可存储、查询、分析和复用的统计表。围绕六项性质（可恢复性、个性化、时间连贯性、可解释性、稳定性和可复用性）用综合质量指数Q进行验证，经典EWMA等标量摘要被证明是该算子类的受限特例。模拟和SoccerMon案例表明该方法优于经典基线和滞后主成分基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向数据分析缺乏一种可复用的中间统计对象，即一个既能概括近期历史、又能像主成分得分或随机效应表那样被存储和重复使用的统计表。现有方法（如EWMA）通常只产生标量摘要，无法捕捉多变量或个性化的记忆结构。

Method: 引入记忆算子将每个掩码窗口化的历史序列映射为有限维状态向量；将多个时间点的状态及其不确定性汇集为潜在记忆表。提出六项验证性质（可恢复性、个性化、时间连贯性、可解释性、稳定性、可复用性）并由综合质量指数Q汇总评估。通过模拟实验检验Q与旋转不变恢复分数对记忆结构的判别力，并以Transformer编码器和SoccerMon案例进行实证。

Result: 模拟实验表明Q和旋转不变恢复分数能区分真实的多元或个性化记忆与阴性对照及错配窗口，而单纯的机制分类准确率不能。SoccerMon案例中潜在记忆表达到Q≈0.73，显著优于经典基线（EWMA等）和滞后主成分基线的约0.40；对部分健康目标有增量留出价值，Procrustes集成提供行级可靠性。

Conclusion: 潜在记忆表值得作为纵向数据中的一等统计对象被接纳，它能超越经典标量摘要的表达能力，为统计工作流提供可复用的记忆性中间表示。其价值需通过系统性的质量框架（六性质+Q指数）来验证，而非仅依赖预测或分类精度。

Abstract: We propose a new unit of analysis for longitudinal data: the Latent Memory Table. The scientific contribution is not the encoder. It is that table, treated as a reusable statistical object on the same footing as a matrix of principal-component scores, a table of estimated random effects, or a table of predicted probabilities. We estimate a statistical table that summarizes recent longitudinal history and is intended to be stored, queried, analysed and reused throughout the statistical workflow. A memory operator maps each masked windowed history to a finite-dimensional state; collecting those states with uncertainty yields the Latent Memory Table. Validation is organized around six properties---recoverability, personalization, temporal coherence, interpretability, stability and reusability---summarized by a composite quality index \(Q\); the Transformer, the SoccerMon case study and the simulations exist to argue that this table deserves that status. Classical exponentially weighted moving averages and related short- and long-horizon scalar summaries arise as restricted, typically univariate special cases of the same operator class. A simulation study with known memory mechanisms shows that \(Q\) and rotation-invariant recovery scores discriminate genuine multivariate or personalized memory from negative controls and from misspecified windows, whereas regime classification accuracy alone does not. SoccerMon serves as an empirical case study: a constructed Latent Memory Table attains \(Q\approx 0.73\) versus about \(0.40\) for classical and lagged principal-component baselines, with incremental held-out value for some wellness targets and Procrustes ensembles for row-wise reliability.

</details>
